namespace AspenTech.Hydraulics.Composition

	internal interface IUnitOpPlac
	{
		bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit);
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public abstract class UnitOpPlac : UnitOpSolver, IUnitOpPlac
	{
		internal bool UnitOpRecreateCompDomain;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH ComputationalDomain;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CBoundaryCondition[] boundaryCondition;

		public abstract int PlacCount { get; }

		public virtual int PlacUnitCount => 1;

		public abstract int SimplePipeCount { get; }

		internal virtual int LastCHMIndex => ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0);

		internal void InitBC(int nBC)
		{
			boundaryCondition = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CBoundaryCondition[nBC];
			for (int i = 0; i < nBC; i++)
			{
				boundaryCondition[i] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CBoundaryCondition();
			}
		}

		internal void setFortranBCs()
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				if ((base.UnitOp as UnitOp).IsPortActive(i))
				{
					boundaryCondition[num].SetFBCType(base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i));
					num++;
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CheckBoundary(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit, bool CalcDisplay, int nphs, double oldPressure, double relaxFactor)
		{
			IPhysicalPropertyPackage physicalPropertyPackage = null;
			FlashCalculationLevelEnum flashCalculationLevelEnum = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.Thermal;
			try
			{
				bool result = true;
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = 0;
				UnitOp unitOp = null;
				HydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase = null;
				IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
				double eMPTY = RealProperty.EMPTY;
				double eMPTY2 = RealProperty.EMPTY;
				double eMPTY3 = RealProperty.EMPTY;
				double eMPTY4 = RealProperty.EMPTY;
				PortDirectionEnum portDirectionEnum = PortDirectionEnum.PortUndefined;
				physicalPropertyPackage = base.UnitOp.PropertyPackage;
				IFlowStream flowStream = null;
				if (physicalPropertyPackage is ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase)
				{
					switch (nphs)
					{
					case 2:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.LinearCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
						break;
					case 3:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (physicalPropertyPackage is ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo)
				{
					switch (nphs)
					{
					case 2:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.LinearCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
						break;
					case 3:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (physicalPropertyPackage is AHPPPWithAspenProperties)
				{
					switch (nphs)
					{
					case 2:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.LinearCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
						break;
					case 3:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
						break;
					}
				}
				unitOp = base.UnitOp as UnitOp;
				num = unitOp.PortCollection.Count;
				int num4 = unitOp.PointValues.GetUpperBound(0);
				if (num > 2)
				{
					num4 = 1;
				}
				int num5 = 2;
				int num6 = 0;
				int num7 = 0;
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < num; num2++)
				{
					portDirectionEnum = unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).PortType;
					if ((!CalcDisplay && portDirectionEnum == PortDirectionEnum.PortUndefined) || !unitOp.IsPortActive(num2))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (num2 == 0)
					{
						num7 = num6;
					}
					if (num2 == 1)
					{
						num7 = num4;
					}
					if (num2 == 2)
					{
						num7 = num5;
					}
					hydraulicFlowStream = unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
					double value = unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).Diameter.Value;
					IUnitOp ConnectedUnitOp = null;
					unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).GetUnitOpConnected(0, ref ConnectedUnitOp);
					IUnitOp ConnectedUnitOp2 = null;
					unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).GetUnitOpConnected(1, ref ConnectedUnitOp2);
					portDirectionEnum = unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).PortType;
					eMPTY4 = (hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.IsUnknown ? unitOp.PointValues[num7].FlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value : hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
					switch (portDirectionEnum)
					{
					case PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity:
						eMPTY = unitOp.PointValues[num7].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
						eMPTY3 = hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
						break;
					case PortDirectionEnum.PortPressure:
						eMPTY2 = hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
						if (eMPTY2 != 10.0)
						{
							eMPTY = ((oldPressure != 0.0) ? (eMPTY2 * relaxFactor + oldPressure * (1.0 - relaxFactor)) : eMPTY2);
							oldPressure = eMPTY;
						}
						else
						{
							eMPTY = oldPressure;
						}
						if (hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0) != null)
						{
							hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value = eMPTY;
						}
						if (hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1) != null)
						{
							hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Pressure.Value = eMPTY;
						}
						eMPTY3 = unitOp.PointValues[num7].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
						break;
					default:
						eMPTY = unitOp.PointValues[num7].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
						eMPTY3 = unitOp.PointValues[num7].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
						break;
					}
					flowStream = unitOp.PointValues[num7].FlowStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
					bool flag = !isInit || (ConnectedUnitOp == null && portDirectionEnum == PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity && hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value > 0.0) || (ConnectedUnitOp2 == null && portDirectionEnum == PortDirectionEnum.PortPressure && (hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.IsUnknown || hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value < 0.0)) || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).ComponentMoleFractions == null || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).ComponentMoleFractions.Count == 0 || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).ComponentMoleFractions[0].Value == RealValue.FEMPTY;
					if (physicalPropertyPackage != null && flag)
					{
						flashCalculationLevelEnum = physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel;
						physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.ThermalAndTransport;
						flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value = eMPTY;
						flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value = eMPTY3;
						if (hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions != null && hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions.Count != 0 && hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions[0].Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							flowStream.CopyStreamComposition(hydraulicFlowStream);
						}
						physicalPropertyPackage.FlashStream(flowStream, FlashType.PTFlash);
						physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = flashCalculationLevelEnum;
						int numberOfPhases = flowStream.NumberOfPhases;
						IPhase phase = null;
						if (numberOfPhases == nphs - 1)
						{
							if (nphs == 2 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Clone() as IPhase;
								phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
								phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
							}
							else if (nphs == 2 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid).Clone() as IPhase;
								phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
								phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, phase);
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid));
							}
							else if (nphs == 3 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous).Clone() as IPhase;
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(2).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(2).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							}
							else if (nphs == 3 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid).Clone() as IPhase;
								double num8 = 0.0;
								IComponent component = null;
								for (num3 = 0; num3 < phase.NumberOfCompounds; num3++)
								{
									component = phase.GetComponent(num3);
									if (component.Alias == "Water")
									{
										num8 = phase.ComponentMoleFractions[num3].Value;
										break;
									}
								}
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(3).PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
								if (num8 > 0.25)
								{
									hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(2).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								}
								else
								{
									hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(3).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
						}
						hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value = eMPTY4;
						physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = flashCalculationLevelEnum;
						if (unitOp.SolverUnitOp is UnitOpPlac && (unitOp.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).boundaryCondition[num2].MycSharpFluid != null)
						{
							(unitOp.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).boundaryCondition[num2].MycSharpFluid.NullifyFluid();
						}
					}
					if (!(unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).FlowStream is IHydraulicFlowStream))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).PortType == PortDirectionEnum.PortPressure)
					{
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
						if (!isInit)
						{
							for (num3 = 1; num3 <= hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases; num3++)
							{
								(hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(num3) as HydraulicPhase).Pressure.Value = hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
							}
							hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, 0.0, PressurePort: true, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
						}
					}
					else if (unitOp.PortCollection.Item(num2).PortType == PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity)
					{
						for (num3 = 1; num3 <= hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases; num3++)
						{
							(hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(num3) as HydraulicPhase).Temperature.Value = hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
						}
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, 0.0, PressurePort: false, VelocityPort: true, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
					}
					else if (!isInit)
					{
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, 0.0, PressurePort: false, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "UnitOpPlac.CheckBoundary"), innerException);
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit)
		{
			IPhysicalPropertyPackage physicalPropertyPackage = null;
			FlashCalculationLevelEnum flashCalculationLevelEnum = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.Thermal;
			try
			{
				bool result = true;
				int num = base.UnitOp.PointValues.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
				int num2 = 0;
				double tAngle = 0.0;
				HydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
				IHydraulicPhase[] array = null;
				if (base.UnitOp is PipeSegment)
				{
					tAngle = (base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).Inclination.Value;
				}
				int numberOfPhases = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream.NumberOfPhases;
				int numberOfCompounds = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).NumberOfCompounds;
				physicalPropertyPackage = base.UnitOp.PropertyPackage;
				if (physicalPropertyPackage is ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase)
				{
					switch (numberOfPhases)
					{
					case 2:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.LinearCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
						break;
					case 3:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (physicalPropertyPackage is ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo)
				{
					switch (numberOfPhases)
					{
					case 2:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.LinearCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
						break;
					case 3:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (physicalPropertyPackage is AHPPPWithAspenProperties)
				{
					switch (numberOfPhases)
					{
					case 2:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.LinearCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
						break;
					case 3:
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).LiquidPhaseCombination = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
						(physicalPropertyPackage as AHPPPWithAspenProperties).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
						break;
					}
				}
				IFlowStream flowStream = null;
				int num3 = 0;
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					num3 = Math.Max(num3, base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).FlowStream.NumberOfPhases);
				}
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < num; num2++)
				{
					double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
					double num4 = value * value * Math.PI * 0.25;
					hydraulicFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream as HydraulicFlowStream;
					bool flag = !isInit || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions == null || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).ComponentMoleFractions == null || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).NumberOfCompounds != numberOfCompounds || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).NumberOfCompounds != numberOfCompounds || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions[0].Value == RealValue.FEMPTY || hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).ComponentMoleFractions[0].Value == RealValue.FEMPTY;
					if ((base.UnitOp is T_Junction || base.UnitOp is Separator) && num2 < 3 && !(base.UnitOp as UnitOp).IsPortActive(num2))
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					IHydraulicPhase[] array2;
					if (physicalPropertyPackage != null && flag)
					{
						double value2 = hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
						flowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
						int num5 = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.NumberOfPhases + 1;
						array2 = new HydraulicPhase[num5];
						array = array2;
						for (int j = 0; j < num5; j++)
						{
							array[j] = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(j) as IHydraulicPhase;
						}
						array[0].PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = 1.0;
						if (!isInit)
						{
							if (base.UnitOp is Separator)
							{
								if (num2 < 3)
								{
									hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, tAngle, PressurePort: false, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
								}
								else
								{
									hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, tAngle, PressurePort: true, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, tAngle, PressurePort: false, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
							}
						}
						flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value = hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
						flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value = hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
						flashCalculationLevelEnum = physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel;
						physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.ThermalAndTransport;
						physicalPropertyPackage.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PTFlash);
						physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = flashCalculationLevelEnum;
						if (flowStream.NumberOfPhases == num3 - 1)
						{
							IPhase phase = null;
							if (num3 == 2 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Clone() as IPhase;
								phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
								phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
							}
							else if (num3 == 2 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid).Clone() as IPhase;
								phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
								phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, phase);
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid));
							}
							else if (num3 == 3 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous).Clone() as IPhase;
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(2).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(2).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							}
							else if (num3 == 3 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous) == null)
							{
								phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid).Clone() as IPhase;
								double num6 = 0.0;
								IComponent component = null;
								for (int i = 0; i < phase.NumberOfCompounds; i++)
								{
									component = phase.GetComponent(i);
									if (component.Alias == "Water")
									{
										num6 = phase.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value;
										break;
									}
								}
								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
								hydraulicFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(3).PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
								if (num6 > 0.25)
								{
									hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(2).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								}
								else
								{
									hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(3).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream);
						}
						hydraulicFlowStream.MassFlowrate.Value = value2;
						if (ComputationalDomain != null && num2 < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.Length && ComputationalDomain[num2].MycSharpFluid != null)
						{
							ComputationalDomain[num2].MycSharpFluid.NullifyFluid();
						}
					}
					int num7 = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.NumberOfPhases + 1;
					array2 = new HydraulicPhase[num7];
					array = array2;
					for (int k = 0; k < num7; k++)
					{
						array[k] = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(k) as IHydraulicPhase;
					}
					array[0].PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = 1.0;
					if (!isInit)
					{
						if (base.UnitOp is Separator)
						{
							if (num2 < 3)
							{
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, tAngle, PressurePort: false, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
							}
							else
							{
								hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, tAngle, PressurePort: true, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							hydraulicFlowStream.SetTwoPhaseFlowRates(value, tAngle, PressurePort: false, VelocityPort: false, prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha);
						}
					}
					for (int l = 0; l < num7; l++)
					{
						if (array[l].Velocity.IsUnknown)
						{
							array[l].Velocity.Value = array[l].MassFlowRate.Value / (array[l].Density.Value * num4 * array[l].PerCentAreaOccupied.Value);
						}
					}
					IInsulationLayer insulationLayer = null;
					if (base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation == null)
					{
						base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].CreateInsulation();
						base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.ExternalFlowStream = new HydraulicFlowStream();
						base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.InternalDiameter = new RealValue();
						base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.AssignExternalHT(Insulation.HeatTransferCalcZero);
					}
					bool flag2 = false;
					if (base.UnitOp is PipeSegment)
					{
						flag2 = !(base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).HeatTransferOnOff;
					}
					else if (base.UnitOp is Source)
					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
					for (int m = 0; m < base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.NLayers; m++)
					{
						insulationLayer = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.InsulationLayers[m];
						if (insulationLayer.Density.IsUnknown)
						{
							insulationLayer.Density.Value = 900.0;
						}
						if (insulationLayer.ExternalTemperature.IsUnknown || !isInit)
						{
							insulationLayer.ExternalTemperature.Value = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.ExternalTemperature.Value;
						}
						if (insulationLayer.InternalTemperature.IsUnknown || !isInit)
						{
							insulationLayer.InternalTemperature.Value = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.InternalTemperature.Value;
						}
						if (insulationLayer.HeatSource.IsUnknown)
						{
							insulationLayer.HeatSource.Value = 0.0;
						}
						if (insulationLayer.Thickness.IsUnknown || insulationLayer.Thickness.Value < 1E-05)
						{
							insulationLayer.Thickness.Value = 0.025;
						}
						if (insulationLayer.SpecificHeat.IsUnknown)
						{
							insulationLayer.SpecificHeat.Value = 4850.0;
						}
						if (insulationLayer.Conductivity.IsUnknown)
						{
							insulationLayer.Conductivity.Value = 0.1;
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							if (m == 0 && !isInit)
							{
								insulationLayer.InternalTemperature.Value = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Temperature.Value;
							}
							insulationLayer.SpecificHeat.Value = 100000000.0;
							insulationLayer.Conductivity.Value = 0.0001;
						}
					}
					if (base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.InternalDiameter.IsUnknown)
					{
						base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.InternalDiameter.Value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "UnitOpPlac.CheckPointValues"), innerException);
			}
		}

		internal virtual void RestoreFGeom(GCHandle[][] gcRefMyFFace, GCHandle[][] gcRefMyFCell, GCHandle[][] gcRefMyPDiameter, GCHandle[][] gcRefMyConstDiameter, int iPlac)
		{
			int simplePipeCount = SimplePipeCount;

			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell[] RefMyFCell = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell[simplePipeCount];

			{

				RefMyFCell[i] = default(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell);
				RefMyPDiameter[i] = default(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PDiameter);
				RefMyConstDiameter[i] = default(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter);

		}

		internal virtual void GCFree()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
			{
				if (ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid != null)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.GCFree();
					ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].MyInsulation.DeallocateFInsulation();
				}
				if (ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[i].Free();
				}
				if (ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[i].Free();
				}
				if (ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide[i].Free();
				}
			}
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; j++)
			{
				if ((base.UnitOp as UnitOp).IsPortActive(j))
				{
					boundaryCondition[num].nullifyFluid();
					num++;
				}
			}
		}

		internal unsafe virtual void RestoreConnection(UnitOpPlac attachPlac)
		{

			int count = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count;
			num = 0;
			num2 = (base.UnitOp as UnitOp).NumberActivePorts();
			for (; num < count; num++)
			{
				if (!(base.UnitOp as UnitOp).IsPortActive(num))
				{
					continue;
				}
				base.UnitOp.GetConnectedUnitOp(num, ref UnitToConnect);
				if (!unitOp.Equals(UnitToConnect))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (num == 2 && num2 != count)
				{
					if ((base.UnitOp as UnitOp).PortConnectionDirection(num) == PortInOutEnum.PortIn)
					{
						array[0] = 0;
					}
					else if ((base.UnitOp as UnitOp).PortConnectionDirection(num) == PortInOutEnum.PortOut)
					{
						array[0] = 1;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					array[0] = num;
				}
				num = count;
			}
			int count2 = unitOp.PortCollection.Count;
			num = 0;
			num2 = (unitOp as UnitOp).NumberActivePorts();
			for (; num < count2; num++)
			{
				if (!(unitOp as UnitOp).IsPortActive(num))
				{
					continue;
				}
				unitOp.GetConnectedUnitOp(num, ref UnitToConnect);
				if (UnitToConnect is MultiSegPipe)
				{
					if (array[0] == 0)
					{
						UnitToConnect = (UnitToConnect as MultiSegPipe).GetPipeSegment(0) as IUnitOp;
					}
					else
					{
						int numberOfSegments = (UnitToConnect as IMultiSegPipe).NumberOfSegments;
						UnitToConnect = (UnitToConnect as MultiSegPipe).GetPipeSegment(numberOfSegments - 1) as IUnitOp;
					}
				}
				if (!base.UnitOp.Equals(UnitToConnect))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (num == 2 && num2 != count2)
				{
					if ((unitOp as UnitOp).PortConnectionDirection(num) == PortInOutEnum.PortIn)
					{
						array[1] = 0;
					}
					else if ((unitOp as UnitOp).PortConnectionDirection(num) == PortInOutEnum.PortOut)
					{
						array[1] = 1;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					array[1] = num;
				}
				num = count2;
			}
			UnitOp unitOp2 = null;
			unitOp2 = ((!(unitOp is MultiSegPipe)) ? (unitOp as UnitOp) : ((array[1] != 0) ? ((unitOp as MultiSegPipe).GetPipeSegment((unitOp as IMultiSegPipe).NumberOfSegments - 1) as UnitOp) : ((unitOp as MultiSegPipe).GetPipeSegment(0) as UnitOp)));
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			UnitOpPlac unitOpPlac = unitOp2.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac;
			if (array[0] == 0)
			{
				num3 = 0;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary->LeftPossible_UnitOpSummary = &(unitOp2.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary->MyPossible_UnitOpSummary;
			}
			else if (array[0] == 1)
			{
				num3 = LastCHMIndex;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary->RightPossible_UnitOpSummary = &(unitOp2.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary->MyPossible_UnitOpSummary;
			}
			else if (array[0] == 2)
			{
				num3 = 3;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary->SidePossible_UnitOpSummary = &(unitOp2.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary->MyPossible_UnitOpSummary;
			}
			if (array[1] == 0)
			{
				num4 = 0;
			}
			else if (array[1] == 1)
			{
				num4 = unitOpPlac.LastCHMIndex;
			}

			}
			else if (array[0] == 0 && array[1] == 1)
			{
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num3].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.LeftCHM = unitOpPlac.ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num4].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.LeftCHM;
			}
			else if (array[0] == 1 && array[1] == 0)
			{
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num3].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.RightCHM = unitOpPlac.ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num4].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.RightCHM;
			}
			else if (array[0] == 0 && array[1] == 2)
			{
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num3].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.LeftCHM = unitOpPlac.ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num4].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.LeftCHM;
			}
			else if (array[0] == 2 && array[1] == 0)
			{
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num3].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.RightCHM = unitOpPlac.ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num4].MyFCHM.MyConnectivity.RightCHM;
