		internal virtual void RestoreAllConnections()
		{
			int count = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if ((base.UnitOp as UnitOp).IsPortActive(i))
				{
					IUnitOp UnitToConnect = null;
					base.UnitOp.GetConnectedUnitOp(i, ref UnitToConnect);
					if (UnitToConnect != null)
					{
						RestoreConnection(UnitToConnect.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal virtual void RestoreStreamFromFStream(GCHandle[][] gcRefMyFFace, GCHandle[][] gcRefMyFCell, int iPlac)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM[] myComputationalGrid = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid;
			int num = myComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			int num2 = 0;
			int i = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			for (int num4 = (base.UnitOp as UnitOp).NumberActivePorts(); i < num4; i++)
			{
				bool velBC = false;
				double area = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value * base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value * Math.PI / 4.0;
				if (boundaryCondition[i].MyFBoundaryCondition.VelocityBC == 0)
				{
					for (num3 = 1; num3 <= boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases; num3++)
					{
						velBC = true;
					}
				}
				num2 = boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.NPhases;
				double[] array = new double[num2];
				int par = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) - 1;
				for (num3 = 0; num3 < num2; num3++)
				{
					array[num3] = ComputationalDomain[par].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].mHoldUp;
				}
				if (boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases == num2 - 1 && boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num2 - 1].mHoldUp > 1E-08)
				{
					double num5 = boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(num2 - 1).MolecularWeight.Value;
					for (num3 = 0; num3 < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; num3++)
					{
						if (ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num3].mStream.NumberOfPhases == num2 && !ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num3].mStream.GetPhaseByID(num2).MolecularWeight.IsUnknown)
						{
							num5 = Math.Max(myComputationalGrid[num3].mStream.GetPhaseByID(num2).MolecularWeight.Value, num5);
						}
					}
					boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = num2;
					IPhase phase = null;
					switch (i)
					{
					case 0:
						phase = myComputationalGrid[0].mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
						if (phase == null)
						{
							phase = myComputationalGrid[0].mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
						}
						break;
					case 1:
						phase = myComputationalGrid[myComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0)].mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
						if (phase == null)
						{
							phase = myComputationalGrid[myComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0)].mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
						}
						break;
					}
					if (phase != null)
					{
						boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(num2, phase);
						if (num2 == 2)
						{
							double num6 = 0.0;
							for (num3 = 0; num3 < num2; num3++)
							{
								num6 += boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].EqMassFraction;
							}
							if (num6 > 1.0 && boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].EqMassFraction >= 0.0 && boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].EqMassFraction <= 1.0)
							{
								boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[1].EqMassFraction = 1.0 - boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].EqMassFraction;
							}
						}
						boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(num2).MolecularWeight.Value = num5;
					}
					else
					{
						boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases = num2 - 1;
					}
				}
				boundaryCondition[i].CopyStreamFromFStreamBC(area, velBC, array);
			}
			for (i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				num2 = myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.NPhases;
				if (myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.NumberOfPhases == num2 - 1 && myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num2 - 1].mHoldUp > 1E-08)
				{
					double num7 = 0.0;
					int j;
					for (j = i - 1; j >= 0; j--)
					{
						if (myComputationalGrid[j].mStream.NumberOfPhases == num2)
						{
							num7 = myComputationalGrid[j].mStream.GetPhaseByID(num2).MolecularWeight.Value;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (num7 == 0.0)
					{
						for (j = i + 1; j <= myComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0); j++)
						{
							if (myComputationalGrid[j].mStream.NumberOfPhases == num2)
							{
								num7 = myComputationalGrid[j].mStream.GetPhaseByID(num2).MolecularWeight.Value;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.NumberOfPhases = num2;
					IPhase phase2 = null;
					if (num7 > 0.0 && j >= 0 && j <= myComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0))
					{
						phase2 = myComputationalGrid[j].mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
					}
					else
					{
						num7 = myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolecularWeight.Value;
						phase2 = myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
					}
					if (phase2 != null)
					{
						myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.SetPhaseValueByID(num2, phase2);
						if (num2 == 2)
						{
							double num8 = 0.0;
							for (num3 = 0; num3 < num2; num3++)
							{
								num8 += myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].EqMassFraction;
							}
							if (num8 > 1.0 && myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].EqMassFraction <= 1.0)
							{
								myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[1].EqMassFraction = 1.0 - myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].EqMassFraction;
							}
						}
						myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.GetPhaseByID(num2).MolecularWeight.Value = num7;
					}
					else
					{
						myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.NumberOfPhases = num2 - 1;
					}
				}
				double num9 = myComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain.Diameter;
				if (myComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face)
				{
					if (gcRefMyFFace[iPlac][i].IsAllocated)
					{
						num9 = ((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace)gcRefMyFFace[iPlac][i].Target).GetDiam();
					}
				}
				else if (myComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell && gcRefMyFCell[iPlac][i].IsAllocated)
				{
					num9 = ((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell)gcRefMyFCell[iPlac][i].Target).GetDiam();
				}
				double area2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * num9 * num9;
				myComputationalGrid[i].CopyStreamFromFStream(ref myComputationalGrid[i].mStream, ref myComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid, area2);
				myComputationalGrid[i].CopyInsulationFromInsulation(ref myComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation, ref myComputationalGrid[i].MyInsulation);
				if (base.UnitOp is PipeSegment)
				{
					if (!(base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).HeatTransferOnOff)
					{
						myComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation.InsulationLayers[0].InternalTemperature.Value = myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Temperature.Value;
					}
				}
				else if (base.UnitOp is Source)
				{
					myComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation.InsulationLayers[0].InternalTemperature.Value = myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.GetPhaseByID(1).Temperature.Value;
				}
				int FlowRegimeValue = 0;
				myComputationalGrid[i].MyFCHM.MyFluid.GetFlowRegime(ref FlowRegimeValue);
				myComputationalGrid[i].mStream.FlowRegime = GetFlowRegimeIndicator(FlowRegimeValue);
			}
		}

		private string GetFlowRegimeIndicator(int RegimeID)
		{
			return RegimeID switch
			{
				99 => "XXXXXX", 
				1 => "GAS", 
				2 => "H-DEN", 
				3 => "H-DIL", 
				4 => "V-BED", 
				5 => "V-DEN", 
				6 => "V-DIL", 
				10 => "H----V", 
				11 => "H----L", 
				12 => "HB----", 
				13 => "H-A---", 
				14 => "H--G--", 
				15 => "H---S-", 
				16 => "H-AG--", 
				17 => "H--GS-", 
				18 => "H-A-S-", 
				19 => "H-AGS-", 
				20 => "V----V", 
				21 => "V----L", 
				22 => "VB----", 
				23 => "V-A---", 
				24 => "V--G--", 
				25 => "V-C---", 
				26 => "V-AG--", 
				27 => "H-AGSD", 
				28 => "H--GSD", 
				29 => "H-A-SD", 
				30 => "H---SD", 
				31 => "H----O", 
				32 => "H----W", 
				33 => "H---OV", 
				34 => "H---VW", 
				35 => "H---OW", 
				36 => "H---SY", 
				37 => "H-A-SY", 
				38 => "H--GSY", 
				39 => "H-AGSY", 
				_ => "------", 
			};
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacPipeSegment : UnitOpPlac, IPlacPipeSegment
	{
		public override int PlacCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = (base.UnitOp as IPointValueGet).NumberOfPointsStored - 1;
				int num2 = num + 1;
				int num3 = 2;
				return num + num2 + num3;
			}
		}

		public override int SimplePipeCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = (base.UnitOp as IPointValueGet).NumberOfPointsStored - 1;
				int num2 = num + 1;
				return num + num2;
			}
		}

		public PlacPipeSegment()
		{
			UnitOpRecreateCompDomain = true;
			InitBC(2);
			base.UnitOpDirty = false;
		}

		public override void RemoveUnitOpCallBack(EventForRealValueHandler cb)
		{
			PipeSegment pipeSegment = base.UnitOp as PipeSegment;
			RealValueWithEvent realValueWithEvent = pipeSegment.Length as RealValueWithEvent;
			realValueWithEvent.EventForRealValue -= cb;
		}

		internal override object CreateUnitOpSummary()
		{
			PipeSegment pipeSegment = base.UnitOp as PipeSegment;
			double value = pipeSegment.Length.Value;
			double value2 = pipeSegment.Height.Value;
			double value3 = pipeSegment.InternalDiameter.Value;
			double value4 = pipeSegment.Roughness.Value;
			return new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_segment(value, value2, value3, value4);
		}

		internal override void AllocateSummaryGCHandle(GCHandle[] gcHandleBasicUnitOp, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, int iPlac)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_segment Simple_segment = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_segment)CreateUnitOpSummary();
			gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(Simple_segment, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary unitOpSummary = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary(ref Simple_segment, ref gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac]);
			gcHandleSummary[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(unitOpSummary, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			int num = (base.UnitOp as IPointValueGet).NumberOfPointsStored - 1;
			double cellLenght = (base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).TotalLenght.Value / (double)num;
			double value = (base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).InternalDiameter.Value;
			double value2 = (base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).Inclination.Value;
			double value3 = (base.UnitOp as PipeSegment).Roughness.Value;
			double internalTemperature = 278.0;
			SetComputationalDomainPipe(num, cellLenght, value, value2, value3, internalTemperature);
		}

		internal void SetComputationalDomainPipe(int NCells, double CellLenght, double InternalDiameter, double Inclination, double Roughness, double InternalTemperature)
		{
			int i = 0;
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; j++)
			{
				boundaryCondition[j].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(j).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
			}
			IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
			if (IsInsulationDirty())
			{
				base.UnitOpDirty = true;
			}
			if (ComputationalDomain == null || base.UnitOpDirty)
			{
				ComputationalDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH(NCells, CellLenght, InternalDiameter, Inclination, Roughness, InternalTemperature);
				bool flag = true;
				IFlowStream flowStream = null;
				for (; i < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].FlowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].Insulation;
						num++;
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						flowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].FlowStream.Clone() as IHydraulicFlowStream;
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream = flowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				base.UnitOpDirty = false;
			}
			else
			{
				for (i = 0; i < 2 * NCells; i++)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[i] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				}
				while (i > 0)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[i] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
					i--;
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool IsInsulationDirty()
		{
			int i = 0;
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = true;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			if (ComputationalDomain != null)
			{
				for (; i < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						num2 = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].Insulation.ExternalTemperature.Value;
						flag = false;
						num++;
						continue;
					}
					num3 = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation.ExternalTemperature.Value;
					flag = true;
					if (num2 != num3)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal override void RestoreFortrandata(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PipeLine[] lclPipeLine, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, FortranMDO[] FixedMDO, int NPhases, int iPlac)
		{
			try
			{
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port = null;
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port2 = null;
				port = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0);
				port2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1);
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].InstanciateFluidFortran(NPhases);
					boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream);
				}
				setFortranBCs();
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].SetFluidForFortranBC();
				}
				UnitOpSolver solverUnitOp = base.UnitOp.SolverUnitOp;
				int upperBound = (solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0);
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[0].MyFCHM.BeginninOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[upperBound].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.RestoreFCHM(gcHandleSummary[iPlac], FixedMDO, NPhases);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string message = ex.Message;
			}
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacValve : PlacFaceFitting, IPlacValve
	{
		public PlacValve()
		{
			string[] array = ValveFactory.AvailableTypes();
			int num = array.Length;
			EnumValues enumValues = new EnumValues();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				enumValues.Add(i, array[i]);
			}
			mSpecificType = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 2);
			mSpecificType.Description = Resources.ValveType;
			SetSpecificProperties();
			BuildDataProperties();
		}

		internal override object CreateUnitOpSummary()
		{
			double num = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ThroatDiameter.Value;
			if (GetPipeFitting() is AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Valve valve)
			{
				num = valve.calculateEquivalentThroatDiameter();
			}
			if (!(base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ModelAreaChangeInDynamics)
			{
				num = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			}
			return new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage(num, num);
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			double num = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ThroatDiameter.Value;
			if (GetPipeFitting() is AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Valve valve)
			{
				num = valve.calculateEquivalentThroatDiameter();
			}
			if (!(base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ModelAreaChangeInDynamics)
			{
				num = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			}
			SetComputationalDomainFitting(num, num);
		}

		public override bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit)
		{
			bool result = base.CheckPointValues(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, isInit);
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve valve = base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve;
			valve.PointValues[2] = valve.PointValues[1];
			return result;
		}

		internal override FittingProperties GetFittingProperties()
		{
			FittingProperties result = null;
			if (mSpecificType != null)
			{
				result = ValveFactory.GetProperties(mSpecificType.Value);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal override PipeFitting GetPipeFitting()
		{
			FittingProperties properties = ValveFactory.GetProperties(mSpecificType.Value);
			FittingProperty commonByName = properties.GetCommonByName("PipeDiam");
			FittingProperty commonByName2 = properties.GetCommonByName("ThroatDiam");
			FittingProperty commonByName3 = properties.GetCommonByName("InletAngle");
			commonByName.Value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			commonByName2.Value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ThroatDiameter.Value;
			commonByName3.Value = 90.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < mSpecificFittingProps.Count; i++)
			{
				FittingProperty specificItem = properties.GetSpecificItem(i);
				if (specificItem != null)
				{
					if (mSpecificFittingProps[i].Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Real)
					{
						specificItem.Value = (mSpecificFittingProps[i] as RealProperty).Value;
					}
					else if (mSpecificFittingProps[i].Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Integer)
					{
						specificItem.Value = (mSpecificFittingProps[i] as IntegerProperty).Value;
					}
				}
			}
			StreamCollection streamCollection = new StreamCollection();
			IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
			IFlowStream flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).FlowStream;
			streamCollection.Add(flowStream);
			streamCollection.Add(flowStream2);
			PipeFitting pipeFitting = ValveFactory.CreateFitting(mSpecificType.Value, properties, streamCollection);
			pipeFitting.MethodPackage = (MethodType)mPressureDropCorr.Value;
			return pipeFitting;
		}

		internal override double GetFrictionFactor()
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (GetPipeFitting() is AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Valve valve)
			{
				double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
				IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
				double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * value * value;
				double massFlux = flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
				if ((base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ModelFormLossInDynamics)
				{
					result = valve.getKFactor(flowStream, massFlux);
				}
				else if ((base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Valve).ModelAreaChangeInDynamics)
				{
					double num2 = valve.calculateEquivalentThroatDiameter();
					result = 1.0 - num2 * num2 / value / value;
					result = 2.0 * result * result;
				}
				else
				{
					result = 0.0;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacTJunction : UnitOpPlac, IPlacTJunction
	{
		public override int PlacCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts() < 3)
				{
					return 2;
				}
				return 4;
			}
		}

		public override int SimplePipeCount => PlacCount;

		internal override int LastCHMIndex
		{
			get
			{
				if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts() < 3)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				return 2;
			}
		}

		public PlacTJunction()
		{
			UnitOpRecreateCompDomain = true;
			InitBC(3);
		}

		internal override void RestoreFortrandata(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PipeLine[] lclPipeLine, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, FortranMDO[] FixedMDO, int NPhases, int iPlac)
		{
			try
			{
				int i = 0;
				int num;
				for (num = (base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts(); i < num; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].InstanciateFluidFortran(NPhases);
					boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream);
				}
				setFortranBCs();
				for (i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].SetFluidForFortranBC();
				}
				UnitOpSolver solverUnitOp = base.UnitOp.SolverUnitOp;
				if (num > 2)
				{
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[0].MyFCHM.BeginninOfGrid = true;
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[2].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[3].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.RestoreFCHM(gcHandleSummary[iPlac], FixedMDO, NPhases);
				}
				else
				{
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[0].MyFCHM.BeginninOfGrid = true;
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[1].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
					(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.RestoreFCHM(gcHandleSummary[iPlac], FixedMDO, NPhases);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "PlacTJunction.RestoreFortrandata"), innerException);
			}
		}

		internal void SetComputationalDomainTJunction()
		{
			T_Junction t_Junction = base.UnitOp as T_Junction;
			double value = t_Junction.BodyDiameter.Value;
			double value2 = t_Junction.PortCollection.Item(2).Diameter.Value;
			double internalTemperature = 278.0;
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
			}
			IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
			if (ComputationalDomain == null || base.UnitOpDirty)
			{
				ComputationalDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH(4);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft = new GCHandle[4];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight = new GCHandle[4];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide = new GCHandle[4];
				bool flag = true;
				while (num < 4)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
						switch (num)
						{
						case 0:
							ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Diameter = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
							break;
						case 2:
							ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Diameter = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
							break;
						default:
							ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value2;
							break;
						}
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Inclination = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Roughness = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature = internalTemperature;
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.DistanceFromBeginningOfUnitOp = 0.0;
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell();
						if (num < 3)
						{
							ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value;
						}
						else
						{
							ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value2;
						}
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Inclination = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.Roughness = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature = internalTemperature;
						ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain.DistanceFromBeginningOfUnitOp = 0.0;
						AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell cell = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell();
						cell = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)ComputationalDomain[num].MyCompDomain;
						cell.Lenght = value * 100.0;
						flag = true;
					}
					num++;
					if (num == 3)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[0].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[0].Insulation;
				int num2 = 1;
				IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				int num4 = 0;
				for (num4 = 1; num4 <= flowStream.NumberOfPhases; num4++)
				{
					num3 += (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(num4) as IHydraulicPhase).MassFlowRate.Value;
				}
				IUnitOp ConnectedUnitOp = null;
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1);
				port.GetUnitOpConnected(1, ref ConnectedUnitOp);
				string lclType = "";
				ConnectedUnitOp.WhatTypeUnitOp(ref lclType);
				if (lclType == "PipeSegment")
				{
					if (port == ConnectedUnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1))
					{
						num3 = 0.0 - num3;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ConnectedUnitOp = null;
					port.GetUnitOpConnected(0, ref ConnectedUnitOp);
					lclType = "";
					ConnectedUnitOp.WhatTypeUnitOp(ref lclType);
					if (lclType == "PipeSegment" && port == ConnectedUnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1))
					{
						num3 = 0.0 - num3;
					}
				}
				if (num3 < 0.0)
				{
					num2 = 2;
					flowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream;
					num3 = 0.0;
					for (num4 = 1; num4 <= flowStream.NumberOfPhases; num4++)
					{
						num3 += (flowStream.GetPhaseByID(num4) as IHydraulicPhase).MassFlowRate.Value;
					}
					ConnectedUnitOp = null;
					AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(2);
					port2.GetUnitOpConnected(0, ref ConnectedUnitOp);
					lclType = "";
					ConnectedUnitOp.WhatTypeUnitOp(ref lclType);
					if (lclType == "PipeSegment")
					{
						if (port2 == ConnectedUnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1))
						{
							num3 = 0.0 - num3;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						ConnectedUnitOp = null;
						port2.GetUnitOpConnected(1, ref ConnectedUnitOp);
						lclType = "";
						ConnectedUnitOp.WhatTypeUnitOp(ref lclType);
						if (lclType == "PipeSegment" && port2 == ConnectedUnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1))
						{
							num3 = 0.0 - num3;
						}
					}
					if (num3 < 0.0)
					{
						num2 = 0;
					}
				}
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[2].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[1].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[2].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[2].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[1].Insulation;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[3].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[2].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[3].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[3].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[2].Insulation;
				base.UnitOpDirty = false;
			}
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM cCHM = null;
			num = 0;
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[1];
			ComputationalDomain[0].ConnectRightCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[0] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[0].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[2];
			ComputationalDomain[1].ConnectRightCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[1].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[1];
			ComputationalDomain[2].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[2] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[2].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[0];
			ComputationalDomain[1].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[1].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[3];
			ComputationalDomain[1].ConnectSideCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide[1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[1].MyCConnectivity.MySideCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[1];
			ComputationalDomain[3].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[3] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[3].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}

		internal void SetComputationalDomainSwage()
		{
			T_Junction t_Junction = base.UnitOp as T_Junction;
			int num = IntegerProperty.EMPTY;
			int num2 = IntegerProperty.EMPTY;
			int num3 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				if (t_Junction.IsPortActive(i))
				{
					if (t_Junction.PortConnectionDirection(i) == PortInOutEnum.PortIn)
					{
						num = i;
					}
					else if (t_Junction.PortConnectionDirection(i) == PortInOutEnum.PortOut)
					{
						num2 = i;
					}
					boundaryCondition[num3].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
					num3++;
				}
			}
			if (ComputationalDomain == null || base.UnitOpDirty)
			{
				num3 = 0;
				IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
				ComputationalDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH(2);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft = new GCHandle[2];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight = new GCHandle[2];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide = new GCHandle[2];
				ComputationalDomain[0].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
				ComputationalDomain[0].MyCompDomain.Diameter = t_Junction.PortCollection.Item(num).Diameter.Value;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[0].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
				ComputationalDomain[1].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
				ComputationalDomain[1].MyCompDomain.Diameter = t_Junction.PortCollection.Item(num2).Diameter.Value;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[0].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
				base.UnitOpDirty = false;
			}
			num3 = 0;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM cCHM = null;
			for (; num3 < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0); num3++)
			{
				cCHM = ComputationalDomain[num3 + 1];
				ComputationalDomain[num3].ConnectRightCHM(ref cCHM);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[num3] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
			for (num3 = 0; num3 < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0); num3++)
			{
				cCHM = ComputationalDomain[num3];
				ComputationalDomain[num3 + 1].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[num3 + 1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[num3 + 1].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
			double swageFrictionFactor = GetSwageFrictionFactor(num, num2);
			ComputationalDomain[0].MyCompDomain.Roughness = swageFrictionFactor;
			ComputationalDomain[1].MyCompDomain.Roughness = swageFrictionFactor;
		}

		internal double GetSwageFrictionFactor(int inPort, int outPort)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Swage swage = null;
			IFlowStream flowStream = null;
			IFlowStream flowStream2 = null;
			double value = ComputationalDomain[0].mStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
			double value2;
			double value3;
			if (value >= 0.0)
			{
				value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(inPort).Diameter.Value;
				value3 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(outPort).Diameter.Value;
				flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(inPort).FlowStream;
				flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(outPort).FlowStream;
			}
			else
			{
				value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(outPort).Diameter.Value;
				value3 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(inPort).Diameter.Value;
				flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(outPort).FlowStream;
				flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(inPort).FlowStream;
			}
			if (value2 > value3)
			{
				swage = new SuddenContraction(value2, value3, flowStream, flowStream2);
			}
			else if (value2 < value3)
			{
				swage = new SuddenEnlargement(value2, value3, flowStream, flowStream2);
			}
			if (swage != null)
			{
				swage.MethodPackage = MethodType.CraneMethod;
			}
			double result = 0.0;
			if (swage != null)
			{
				double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * value2 * value2;
				double massFlux = flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
				result = swage.getKFactor(flowStream, massFlux);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			T_Junction t_Junction = base.UnitOp as T_Junction;
			if (t_Junction.NumberActivePorts() < 3)
			{
				SetComputationalDomainSwage();
			}
			else
			{
				SetComputationalDomainTJunction();
			}
		}

		internal override object CreateUnitOpSummary()
		{
			if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts() < 3)
			{
				double num = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				double num2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).IsPortActive(i))
					{
						if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							num = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).Diameter.Value;
						}
						else if (num2 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							num2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).Diameter.Value;
						}
					}
				}
				return new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage(num, num2);
			}
			return default(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_T_Junction);
		}

		internal override void AllocateCHMGCHandle(GCHandle[] gcHandlePipeLineFirstCHM, GCHandle[] gcHandlePipeLineFirstCHM_BC, GCHandle[] gcHandlePipeLineLastCHM, GCHandle[] gcHandlePipeLineLastCHM_BC, GCHandle[] gcHandlePipeLineSideCHM, GCHandle[] gcHandlePipeLineSideCHM_BC, int iPlac)
		{
			base.AllocateCHMGCHandle(gcHandlePipeLineFirstCHM, gcHandlePipeLineFirstCHM_BC, gcHandlePipeLineLastCHM, gcHandlePipeLineLastCHM_BC, gcHandlePipeLineSideCHM, gcHandlePipeLineSideCHM_BC, iPlac);
			if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts() > 2)
			{
				gcHandlePipeLineSideCHM[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[3].MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				gcHandlePipeLineSideCHM_BC[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(boundaryCondition[2].MyFBoundaryCondition, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
		}

		internal override void AllocateSummaryGCHandle(GCHandle[] gcHandleBasicUnitOp, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, int iPlac)
		{
			if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts() > 2)
			{
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_T_Junction MySimple_T_Junction = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_T_Junction)CreateUnitOpSummary();
				gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(MySimple_T_Junction, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary unitOpSummary = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary(ref MySimple_T_Junction, ref gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac]);
				gcHandleSummary[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(unitOpSummary, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
			else
			{
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage RefSimple_Swage = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage)CreateUnitOpSummary();
				gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(RefSimple_Swage, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary unitOpSummary2 = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary(ref RefSimple_Swage, ref gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac]);
				gcHandleSummary[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(unitOpSummary2, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
		}

		internal override void SetBoundaryMDO(FortranMDO[] FixedMDOUnitOp)
		{
			if ((base.UnitOp as T_Junction).NumberActivePorts() > 2)
			{
				boundaryCondition[0].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[0];
				boundaryCondition[1].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[PlacCount - 2];
				boundaryCondition[2].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[PlacCount - 1];
			}
			else
			{
				boundaryCondition[0].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[0];
				boundaryCondition[1].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[PlacCount - 1];
			}
		}

		public override bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit)
		{
			return base.CheckPointValues(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, isInit);
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public abstract class PlacFaceFitting : UnitOpPlac
	{
		protected EnumProperty mPressureDropCorr;

		protected Properties mSpecificFittingProps;

		public override int PlacCount => (base.UnitOp as IPointValueGet).NumberOfPointsStored;

		public override int SimplePipeCount => PlacCount;

		public PlacFaceFitting()
		{
			UnitOpRecreateCompDomain = true;
			InitBC(2);
			SetPressureDropEnum();
			SetSpecificProperties();
			BuildDataProperties();
		}

		internal virtual void SetPressureDropEnum()
		{
			int num = 3;
			string[] array = new string[num];
			int[] array2 = new int[num];
			EnumValues enumValues = new EnumValues();
			array[0] = Resources.Crane2Phase;
			array[1] = Resources.CraneHomogeneous;
			array[2] = Resources.HTFSMethod;
			array2[0] = 2;
			array2[1] = 1;
			array2[2] = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				enumValues.Add(array2[i], array[i]);
			}
			mPressureDropCorr = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 0);
			mPressureDropCorr.Description = Resources.PressureDropMethod;
		}

		internal virtual void BuildDataProperties()
		{
			Properties properties = base.UnitOpSolverData as Properties;
			while (properties.Count > 0)
			{
				properties.Remove(properties.Count - 1);
			}
			base.UnitOpSolverData.Add(mPressureDropCorr);
			if (mSpecificFittingProps != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < mSpecificFittingProps.Count; i++)
				{
					base.UnitOpSolverData.Add(mSpecificFittingProps.GetItem(i));
				}
			}
		}

		public override void CopyProperties(UnitOpSolver toCopy)
		{
			if (toCopy == null || toCopy == this)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (mSpecificType != null && toCopy.UnitOpSolverType != null)
			{
				mSpecificType.Value = toCopy.UnitOpSolverType.Value;
				SetSpecificProperties();
			}
			Properties properties = toCopy.UnitOpSolverData as Properties;
			for (int i = 0; i < properties.Count; i++)
			{
				Property item = toCopy.UnitOpSolverData.GetItem(i);
				if (item == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string description = item.Description;
				if (item.Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Enumeration && (description == Resources.PressureDropMethod || description == Resources.CalculationMethod))
				{
					int num = (item as EnumProperty).Value;
					if (num == 12 || num == 19 || num == 18)
					{
						num = 0;
					}
					mPressureDropCorr.Value = num;
				}
				if (mSpecificFittingProps == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < mSpecificFittingProps.Count; j++)
				{
					Property item2 = mSpecificFittingProps.GetItem(j);
					if (description == item2.Description)
					{
						if (item.Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Real)
						{
							(item2 as RealProperty).Value = (item as RealProperty).Value;
						}
						else if (item.Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Integer)
						{
							(item2 as IntegerProperty).Value = (item as IntegerProperty).Value;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal virtual FittingProperties GetFittingProperties()
		{
			return null;
		}

		internal void SetSpecificProperties()
		{
			FittingProperties fittingProperties = GetFittingProperties();
			if (fittingProperties == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (mSpecificFittingProps == null)
			{
				mSpecificFittingProps = new Properties();
			}
			else
			{
				mSpecificFittingProps.Clear();
			}
			int num = fittingProperties.NumSpecificProps();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				Property property = null;
				FittingProperty specificItem = fittingProperties.GetSpecificItem(i);
				if (specificItem != null)
				{
					if (specificItem.PropType == 0)
					{
						int value = (int)specificItem.Value;
						property = new IntegerProperty(value);
						property.Description = specificItem.Description;
					}
					else if (specificItem.PropType == 1)
					{
						double value2 = specificItem.Value;
						property = new RealProperty(value2);
						property.Description = specificItem.Description;
					}
					if (property != null)
					{
						mSpecificFittingProps.Add(property);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal override void RestoreFortrandata(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PipeLine[] lclPipeLine, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, FortranMDO[] FixedMDO, int NPhases, int iPlac)
		{
			try
			{
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port = null;
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port2 = null;
				port = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0);
				port2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1);
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].InstanciateFluidFortran(NPhases);
					boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream);
				}
				setFortranBCs();
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].SetFluidForFortranBC();
				}
				int num = (base.UnitOp as IPointValueGet).NumberOfPointsStored - 1;
				UnitOpSolver solverUnitOp = base.UnitOp.SolverUnitOp;
				num = (solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0);
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[0].MyFCHM.BeginninOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[num].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.RestoreFCHM(gcHandleSummary[iPlac], FixedMDO, NPhases);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "PlacFaceFitting.RestoreFortrandata"), innerException);
			}
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			double value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			SetComputationalDomainFitting(value, value2);
		}

		internal void SetComputationalDomainFitting(double InletDiameter, double OutletDiameter)
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
			}
			if (ComputationalDomain == null || base.UnitOpDirty)
			{
				num = 0;
				IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
				ComputationalDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH(2);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft = new GCHandle[2];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight = new GCHandle[2];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide = new GCHandle[2];
				ComputationalDomain[0].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
				ComputationalDomain[0].MyCompDomain.Diameter = InletDiameter;
				ComputationalDomain[1].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
				ComputationalDomain[1].MyCompDomain.Diameter = OutletDiameter;
				for (num = 0; num < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; num++)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num].FlowStream;
					hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
					hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
					ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[num].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[0].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
				}
				base.UnitOpDirty = false;
			}
			num = 0;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM cCHM = null;
			for (; num < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0); num++)
			{
				cCHM = ComputationalDomain[num + 1];
				ComputationalDomain[num].ConnectRightCHM(ref cCHM);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[num] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[num].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
			for (num = 0; num < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0); num++)
			{
				cCHM = ComputationalDomain[num];
				ComputationalDomain[num + 1].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[num + 1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[num + 1].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
			double frictionFactor = GetFrictionFactor();
			ComputationalDomain[0].MyCompDomain.Roughness = frictionFactor;
			ComputationalDomain[1].MyCompDomain.Roughness = frictionFactor;
		}

		internal abstract PipeFitting GetPipeFitting();

		internal abstract double GetFrictionFactor();

		public override bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit)
		{
			return base.CheckPointValues(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, isInit);
		}

		internal override object CreateUnitOpSummary()
		{
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			double value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			return new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage(value, value2);
		}

		internal override void AllocateSummaryGCHandle(GCHandle[] gcHandleBasicUnitOp, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, int iPlac)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage RefSimple_Swage = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage)CreateUnitOpSummary();
			gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(RefSimple_Swage, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary unitOpSummary = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary(ref RefSimple_Swage, ref gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac]);
			gcHandleSummary[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(unitOpSummary, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacSwage : PlacFaceFitting, IPlacSwage
	{
		internal override PipeFitting GetPipeFitting()
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Swage swage = null;
			IFlowStream flowStream = null;
			IFlowStream flowStream2 = null;
			double value = ComputationalDomain[0].mStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
			double value2;
			double value3;
			if (value >= 0.0)
			{
				value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
				value3 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
				flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
				flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).FlowStream;
			}
			else
			{
				value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
				value3 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
				flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).FlowStream;
				flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
			}
			double value4 = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Swage).Angle.Value;
			double num = ((value2 > value3) ? (value3 / value2) : (value2 / value3));
			if (value2 > value3)
			{
				swage = ((!(value4 > 0.0) || !(value4 < 180.0)) ? ((Contraction)new SuddenContraction(value2, value3, flowStream, flowStream2)) : ((Contraction)new GradualContraction(value2, value3, value4, flowStream, flowStream2)));
			}
			else if (value2 < value3)
			{
				swage = ((!(value4 > 0.0) || !(value4 < 180.0)) ? ((Enlargement)new SuddenEnlargement(value2, value3, flowStream, flowStream2)) : ((Enlargement)new GradualEnlargement(value2, value3, value4, flowStream, flowStream2)));
			}
			if (swage != null)
			{
				swage.MethodPackage = (MethodType)mPressureDropCorr.Value;
			}
			return swage;
		}

		internal override double GetFrictionFactor()
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (GetPipeFitting() is AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Swage { UpstreamPipeDiameter: var upstreamPipeDiameter } swage)
			{
				IFlowStream stream = swage.GetPort(0).Stream;
				double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * upstreamPipeDiameter * upstreamPipeDiameter;
				double massFlux = stream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
				result = swage.getKFactor(stream, massFlux);
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacOrifice : PlacFaceFitting, IPlacOrifice
	{
		internal override object CreateUnitOpSummary()
		{
			double value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ThroatDiameter.Value;
			if (!(base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ModelAreaChangeInDynamics)
			{
				value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			}
			return new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Swage(value, value);
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			double value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ThroatDiameter.Value;
			if (!(base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ModelAreaChangeInDynamics)
			{
				value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			}
			SetComputationalDomainFitting(value, value);
		}

		public override bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit)
		{
			bool result = base.CheckPointValues(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, isInit);
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice orifice = base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice;
			orifice.PointValues[2] = orifice.PointValues[1];
			return result;
		}

		internal override PipeFitting GetPipeFitting()
		{
			double value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ThroatDiameter.Value;
			double value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			double value3 = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).PlateThickness.Value;
			IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
			IFlowStream flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).FlowStream;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Orifice orifice = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Orifice(value2, value, value3, flowStream, flowStream2);
			orifice.MethodPackage = (MethodType)mPressureDropCorr.Value;
			return orifice;
		}

		internal override double GetFrictionFactor()
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (GetPipeFitting() is AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Orifice orifice)
			{
				double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
				IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
				double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * value * value;
				double massFlux = flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num;
				if ((base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ModelFormLossInDynamics)
				{
					result = orifice.getKFactor(flowStream, massFlux);
				}
				else if ((base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ModelAreaChangeInDynamics)
				{
					double value2 = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Orifice).ThroatDiameter.Value;
					result = 1.0 - value2 * value2 / value / value;
					result = 2.0 * result * result;
				}
				else
				{
					result = 0.0;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacBend : PlacFaceFitting, IPlacBend
	{
		public PlacBend()
		{
			string[] array = BendFactory.AvailableTypes();
			int num = array.Length;
			EnumValues enumValues = new EnumValues();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				enumValues.Add(i, array[i]);
			}
			mSpecificType = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 0);
			mSpecificType.Description = Resources.BendType;
			SetSpecificProperties();
			BuildDataProperties();
		}

		internal override FittingProperties GetFittingProperties()
		{
			FittingProperties result = null;
			if (mSpecificType != null)
			{
				result = BendFactory.GetProperties(mSpecificType.Value);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal override PipeFitting GetPipeFitting()
		{
			FittingProperties properties = BendFactory.GetProperties(mSpecificType.Value);
			FittingProperty commonByName = properties.GetCommonByName("Angle");
			FittingProperty commonByName2 = properties.GetCommonByName("Radius");
			FittingProperty commonByName3 = properties.GetCommonByName("Diameter");
			commonByName.Value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Bend).Angle.Value;
			commonByName2.Value = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Bend).Radius.Value;
			commonByName3.Value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			for (int i = 0; i < mSpecificFittingProps.Count; i++)
			{
				FittingProperty specificItem = properties.GetSpecificItem(i);
				if (specificItem != null)
				{
					if (mSpecificFittingProps[i].Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Real)
					{
						specificItem.Value = (mSpecificFittingProps[i] as RealProperty).Value;
					}
					else if (mSpecificFittingProps[i].Type == AspenTech.Hydraulics.Data.PropertyType.Integer)
					{
						specificItem.Value = (mSpecificFittingProps[i] as IntegerProperty).Value;
					}
				}
			}
			StreamCollection streamCollection = new StreamCollection();
			IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
			IFlowStream flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).FlowStream;
			streamCollection.Add(flowStream);
			streamCollection.Add(flowStream2);
			PipeFitting pipeFitting = BendFactory.CreateFitting(mSpecificType.Value, properties, streamCollection);
			pipeFitting.MethodPackage = (MethodType)mPressureDropCorr.Value;
			return pipeFitting;
		}

		internal override double GetFrictionFactor()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			PipeFitting pipeFitting = GetPipeFitting();
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * value * value;
			double massFlux = flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value / num2;
			(pipeFitting as AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.Bend).CalculateKAndf(flowStream, massFlux, out var K, out var _);
			return K;
		}
	}

		internal override void AllocateSummaryGCHandle(GCHandle[] gcHandleBasicUnitOp, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, int iPlac)

			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Separator MySimple_Separator = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_Separator)CreateUnitOpSummary();
			gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(MySimple_Separator, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary unitOpSummary = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary(ref MySimple_Separator, ref gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac]);
			gcHandleSummary[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(unitOpSummary, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}

		internal override void SetBoundaryMDO(FortranMDO[] FixedMDOUnitOp)
		{
			boundaryCondition[0].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[0];
			boundaryCondition[1].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[PlacCount - 2];
			boundaryCondition[2].MyFBoundaryCondition.MyMDO = FixedMDOUnitOp[PlacCount - 1];
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			Separator separator = base.UnitOp as Separator;
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			double value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			double value3 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(2).Diameter.Value;
			double num = value2;
			double value4 = separator.Diameter.Value;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double value5 = separator.LiquidLevel.Value;
			double internalTemperature = 278.15;
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 2;
			int num5 = IntegerProperty.EMPTY;
			int num6 = IntegerProperty.EMPTY;
			int num7 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				if (separator.IsPortActive(i))
				{
					if (separator.PortConnectionDirection(i) == PortInOutEnum.PortIn)
					{
						num5 = i;
					}
					else if (separator.PortConnectionDirection(i) == PortInOutEnum.PortOut)
					{
						num6 = i;
					}
					boundaryCondition[num7].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(i).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
					num7++;
				}
			}
			boundaryCondition[2].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[3].FlowStream;
			double value6 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(num5).Diameter.Value;
			double value7 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(num6).Diameter.Value;
			IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
			if (ComputationalDomain == null || base.UnitOpDirty)
			{
				ComputationalDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH(num4 + 2);
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft = new GCHandle[num4 + 2];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight = new GCHandle[num4 + 2];
				ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide = new GCHandle[num4 + 2];
				bool flag = true;
				while (num3 < num4 + 2)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
						switch (num3)
						{
						case 0:
							ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value6;
							break;
						case 2:
							ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value7;
							break;
						default:
							ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value7;
							break;
						}
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Inclination = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Roughness = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature = internalTemperature;
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.DistanceFromBeginningOfUnitOp = 0.0;
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell();
						if (num3 < 3)
						{
							ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Diameter = value6;
						}
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Inclination = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.Roughness = 0.0;
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature = internalTemperature;
						ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain.DistanceFromBeginningOfUnitOp = 0.0;
						AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell cell = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)ComputationalDomain[num3].MyCompDomain;
						cell.Lenght = value6 * 100.0;
						flag = true;
					}
					num3++;
					if (num3 == 3)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num5].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[0].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num5].Insulation;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num5].FlowStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[1].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num5].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[2].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num6].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[2].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[2].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num6].Insulation;
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[3].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[3].FlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[3].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
				hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
				ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[3].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[3].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
				base.UnitOpDirty = false;
			}
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM cCHM = null;
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[1];
			ComputationalDomain[0].ConnectRightCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[0] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[0].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[2];
			ComputationalDomain[1].ConnectRightCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[1].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[1];
			ComputationalDomain[2].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[2] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[2].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[0];
			ComputationalDomain[1].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[1].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[3];
			ComputationalDomain[1].ConnectSideCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleSide[1] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[1].MyCConnectivity.MySideCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			cCHM = ComputationalDomain[1];
			ComputationalDomain[3].ConnectLeftCHM(ref cCHM);
			ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[3] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain[3].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}

		internal override void RestoreFortrandata(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PipeLine[] lclPipeLine, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, FortranMDO[] FixedMDO, int NPhases, int iPlac)
		{
			try
			{
				int i = 0;
				int num;
				for (num = (base.UnitOp as Separator).NumberActivePorts(); i < num; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].InstanciateFluidFortran(NPhases);
					boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream);
				}
				setFortranBCs();
				for (i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].SetFluidForFortranBC();
				}
				boundaryCondition[2].InstanciateFluidFortran(NPhases);
				boundaryCondition[2].PressureBC();
				boundaryCondition[2].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(boundaryCondition[2].mFlowStream);
				boundaryCondition[2].SetFluidForFortranBC();
				UnitOpSolver solverUnitOp = base.UnitOp.SolverUnitOp;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[0].MyFCHM.BeginninOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[2].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[3].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.RestoreFCHM(gcHandleSummary[iPlac], FixedMDO, NPhases);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "PlacSeparator.RestoreFortrandata"), innerException);
			}
		}

		public override bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha, bool isInit)
		{
			int num = base.UnitOp.PointValues[3].FlowStream.NumberOfPhases + 1;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				base.UnitOp.PointValues[3].FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i).Pressure.Value = 101325.0;
			}
			return base.CheckPointValues(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, isInit);
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacFlareTip : PlacFaceFitting, IPlacFlareTip
	{
		protected RealProperty mFrictionFactor;

		internal override void SetPressureDropEnum()
		{
			int num = 3;
			string[] array = new string[num];
			int[] array2 = new int[num];
			EnumValues enumValues = new EnumValues();
			array[0] = Resources.PressureCurveCalc;
			array[1] = Resources.FFTotal;
			array[2] = Resources.FFStatic;
			array2 = new int[num];
			array2[0] = 15;
			array2[1] = 17;
			array2[2] = 16;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				enumValues.Add(array2[i], array[i]);
			}
			mPressureDropCorr = new EnumProperty(enumValues, array2[0]);
			mPressureDropCorr.Description = Resources.PressureDropMethod;
		}

		internal override void BuildDataProperties()
		{
			Properties properties = base.UnitOpSolverData as Properties;
			while (properties.Count > 0)
			{
				properties.Remove(properties.Count - 1);
			}
			base.UnitOpSolverData.Add(mPressureDropCorr);
			mFrictionFactor = new RealProperty(0.0, 0.0);
			mFrictionFactor.Description = Resources.FrictionFactor;
			base.UnitOpSolverData.Add(mFrictionFactor);
			if (mSpecificFittingProps != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < mSpecificFittingProps.Count; i++)
				{
					base.UnitOpSolverData.Add(mSpecificFittingProps.GetItem(i));
				}
			}
		}

		internal override PipeFitting GetPipeFitting()
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.FlareTip flareTip = null;
			IFlowStream flowStream = null;
			IFlowStream flowStream2 = null;
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
			double value2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
			flowStream2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).FlowStream;
			flareTip = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.FlareTip();
			flareTip.GetPort(0).Stream = flowStream;
			flareTip.GetPort(1).Stream = flowStream2;
			flareTip.GetPort(0).Diameter.Value = value;
			flareTip.GetPort(1).Diameter.Value = value2;
			flareTip.TipDiameter = value;
			flareTip.UserFrictionFactor = mFrictionFactor.Value;
			flareTip.PressureDropCurves = (base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FlareTip).PressureDropCurves;
			flareTip.MethodPackage = (MethodType)mPressureDropCorr.Value;
			return flareTip;
		}

		internal override double GetFrictionFactor()
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (GetPipeFitting() is AspenTech.Hydraulics.PipeFittingServer.FlareTip flareTip)
			{
				if (flareTip.MethodPackage == MethodType.StaticFrictionFactorMethod || flareTip.MethodPackage == MethodType.TotalFrictionFactorMethod)
				{
					result = flareTip.UserFrictionFactor;
				}
				else if (flareTip.MethodPackage == MethodType.PressureCurveMethod)
				{
					IFlowStream flowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).FlowStream;
					double value = flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
					double value2 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolecularWeight.Value;
					AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FlareTip flareTip2 = base.UnitOp as AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FlareTip;
					if (flareTip2.PressureDropCurves != null)
					{
						double pressureDrop = flareTip2.PressureDropCurves.GetPressureDrop(value2, value);
						double value3 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0).Diameter.Value;
						double num = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(value3, 2.0);
						double num2 = value / num;
						double value4 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).Density.Value;
						result = 2.0 * value4 * Math.Abs(pressureDrop) / num2 / num2;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class PlacSource : UnitOpPlac
	{
		public override int PlacCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 1;
				int num2 = 2;
				int num3 = 2;
				return num + num2 + num3;
			}
		}

		public override int SimplePipeCount
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 1;
				int num2 = 2;
				return num + num2;
			}
		}

		public PlacSource()
		{
			UnitOpRecreateCompDomain = true;
			InitBC(2);
			base.UnitOpDirty = false;
		}

		internal double GetValveLengthForDyn()
		{
			double val = 0.0;
			IUnitOp UnitToConnect = null;
			base.UnitOp.GetConnectedUnitOp(1, ref UnitToConnect);
			if (UnitToConnect is PipeSegment pipeSegment)
			{
				val = pipeSegment.Length.Value / (double)(pipeSegment.NumberOfPointsStored - 1);
			}
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			return Math.Min(val, 10.0 * value);
		}

		internal override object CreateUnitOpSummary()
		{
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			double valveLengthForDyn = GetValveLengthForDyn();
			double height = 0.0;
			double roughness = 0.0;
			return new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_segment(valveLengthForDyn, height, value, roughness);
		}

		internal override void AllocateSummaryGCHandle(GCHandle[] gcHandleBasicUnitOp, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, int iPlac)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_segment Simple_segment = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary.Simple_segment)CreateUnitOpSummary();
			gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(Simple_segment, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary unitOpSummary = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary(ref Simple_segment, ref gcHandleBasicUnitOp[iPlac]);
			gcHandleSummary[iPlac] = GCHandle.Alloc(unitOpSummary, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}

		internal override void SetComputationalDomain()
		{
			int num = 1;
			double value = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1).Diameter.Value;
			double valveLengthForDyn = GetValveLengthForDyn();
			double inclination = 0.0;
			double roughenss = 0.0;
			double internalTemperature = 278.0;
			int i = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; j++)
			{
				boundaryCondition[j].mFlowStream = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(j).FlowStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
			}
			IHydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
			if (ComputationalDomain == null || base.UnitOpDirty)
			{
				ComputationalDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CUOH(num, valveLengthForDyn, value, inclination, roughenss, internalTemperature);
				bool flag = true;
				IFlowStream flowStream = null;
				for (; i < ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation;
						num2++;
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						flowStream = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].FlowStream.Clone() as IHydraulicFlowStream;
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream = flowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream = ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream as IHydraulicFlowStream;
						hydraulicFlowStream.SetMassFlowRateCallBack();
						ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation = base.UnitOp.PointValues[num2].Insulation.Clone() as IInsulation;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				base.UnitOpDirty = false;
			}
			else
			{
				for (i = 0; i < 2 * num; i++)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleRight[i] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				}
				while (i > 0)
				{
					ComputationalDomain.GCHandleLeft[i] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
					i--;
				}
			}
			if ((base.UnitOp as Source).FlangeDiameter.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
			{
				double value2 = (base.UnitOp as Source).FlangeDiameter.Value;
				ComputationalDomain.GetFirstFace().MyCompDomain.Diameter = value2;
			}
		}

		internal override void RestoreFortrandata(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PipeLine[] lclPipeLine, GCHandle[] gcHandleSummary, FortranMDO[] FixedMDO, int NPhases, int iPlac)
		{
			try
			{
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port = null;
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port2 = null;
				port = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(0);
				port2 = base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Item(1);
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].InstanciateFluidFortran(NPhases);
					boundaryCondition[i].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(boundaryCondition[i].mFlowStream);
				}
				setFortranBCs();
				for (int i = 0; i < base.UnitOp.PortCollection.Count; i++)
				{
					boundaryCondition[i].SetFluidForFortranBC();
				}
				UnitOpSolver solverUnitOp = base.UnitOp.SolverUnitOp;
				int upperBound = (solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0);
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[0].MyFCHM.BeginninOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain[upperBound].MyFCHM.EndOfGrid = true;
				(solverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).ComputationalDomain.RestoreFCHM(gcHandleSummary[iPlac], FixedMDO, NPhases);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string message = ex.Message;
			}
		}
	}

				lclupper_index = -32767;

				lclPOILWT = 1.0;
				lclIFFM = 1;
				lclNFREQ = 1;
				lclPTRANS = 1000.0;
				lclGasLiftID = -1;

				lclIDAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
				lclMWAddress = Empty.emptyPtr;
				lclMWAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
				lclCompMode = 0;
				lclCompTcrit = 0.1;
				lclCompPcrit = 0.1;
				lclCompCcrit = 0.01;
			}

			public void Initialise()
			{
				lclNADINT = 100;
				lclMXAUTO = 10;
				lclARFRCT = 0.5;
				lclRECFTC = 1.5;
				lclEPSO = 0.1;
				lclOITMAX = 10;
				lclFIFG = 0.0;
				lclMAXVOL = 0.995;
				lclTIMET = 0.0;
				lclNPHASE = 2;
				lclIENERGY = 1;
				lclIPHASE = 0;
				lclRXTRPH = 0.02;
				lclDISSIP = 0;
				lclTRANSI = 1;
				lclICFLOW = 0;
				lclFRICFLAG = 2;
				lclStatus = 0;
				lclDTMIN = 1E-05;
				lclDTMAX = 0.5;
				lclTEND = 30.0;
				lclDelt = -32767.0;
				lclEDINT = 2.0;
				lclRECINT = 1.0;
				lclComponent_Number = -32767;
				lcllower_index = -32767;
				lclupper_index = -32767;
				lclNVISC = 3;
				lclPINVER = 0.5;
				lclNOILWT = 3;
				lclSLIPMODE = 0;
				lclPOILWT = 1.0;
				lclIFFM = 1;
				lclNFREQ = 1;
				lclPTRANS = 1000.0;
				lclGasLiftID = -1;
				lclNCPDS = -32767;
				lclIDAddress = Empty.emptyPtr;
				lclIDAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
				lclMWAddress = Empty.emptyPtr;
				lclMWAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
				lclCompMode = 0;
				lclCompTcrit = 0.1;
				lclCompPcrit = 0.1;
				lclCompCcrit = 0.01;
			}

			internal void SetIDAddress(IntPtr IDAddressValue)
			{
				lclIDAddress = IDAddressValue;
			}

			internal void SetMWAddress(IntPtr MWAddressValue)
			{
				lclMWAddress = MWAddressValue;
			}
		}

		public int NetworkIdentifier
		{
			get
			{
				if (lclNetworkID == -32767)
				{
					lclNetworkID = 0;
				}
				return lclNetworkID;
			}
			set
			{
				if (0 > value || value > 999999)
				{
					throw new Exception(Resources.InvalidNetworkID);
				}
				lclNetworkID = value;
			}
		}
