		public CompositionSolver()
		{
			try
			{
				SetEnvironmentVariables();
				IsSet = false;
				isInitalised = false;
				RecreateCompDomain = true;
				base.NetworkDirty = false;
				RealProperty realProperty = null;
				EnumProperty enumProperty = null;
				IntegerProperty integerProperty = null;
				EnumValues enumValues = null;
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = 0;
				string[] array = null;
				int[] array2 = null;
				int errCode = 0;
				mActiveUnitOps = new UnitOpCollection();
				mIsBlackOilSupported = false;
				mIsCompositionalSupported = true;
				mIsPVTTableSupported = false;
				mPhasesSupported = 2;
				LocalCopyOfPlacData = new FPlac(1);
				LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNPHASE = base.PhasesSupported;
				LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclCompMode = 1;
				LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclIPHASE = 0;
				LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNCPDS = -32767;
				lclIPPPCollection = new IPPPCollection();
				checkData = true;
				lclDelt = new RealProperty();
				solverResults.Add(lclDelt);
				solverResults[0].Description = Resources.UsedTimeStep;
				realProperty = solverResults[0] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.VarType = "Time";
				realProperty.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				propertyCollection = new Properties();
				lclNADINT = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclNADINT);
				propertyCollection[0].Description = Resources.AutoRecoverySnapshotInterval;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[0] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setNADINT;
				realProperty.Value = 100.0;
				realProperty.UBound = 200.0;
				realProperty.LBound = 0.1;
				lclMXAUTO = new IntegerProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclMXAUTO);
				propertyCollection[1].Description = Resources.MaxAutoRecoveryAttempts;
				integerProperty = propertyCollection[1] as IntegerProperty;
				integerProperty.AfterValueChanged += setMXAUTO;
				integerProperty.Value = 10;
				integerProperty.UBound = 100.0;
				integerProperty.LBound = 1.0;
				lclARFRCT = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclARFRCT);
				propertyCollection[2].Description = Resources.AutoRecoveryDecreaseFactor;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[2] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setARFRCT;
				realProperty.Value = 0.5;
				realProperty.UBound = 0.9;
				realProperty.LBound = 0.1;
				lclRECFTC = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclRECFTC);
				propertyCollection[3].Description = Resources.AutoRecoveryIncreaseFactor;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[3] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setRECFTC;
				realProperty.Value = 1.5;
				realProperty.UBound = 5.0;
				realProperty.LBound = 1.1;
				lclEPSO = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclEPSO);
				propertyCollection[4].Description = Resources.OuterLoopTol;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[4] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setEPSO;
				realProperty.Value = 0.1;
				realProperty.UBound = 1.0;
				realProperty.LBound = 0.0001;
				lclOITMAX = new IntegerProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclOITMAX);
				propertyCollection[5].Description = Resources.MaxOuterLoopIter;
				integerProperty = propertyCollection[5] as IntegerProperty;
				integerProperty.AfterValueChanged += setOITMAX;
				integerProperty.Value = 10;
				integerProperty.UBound = 10.0;
				integerProperty.LBound = 1.0;
				lclFIFG = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclFIFG);
				propertyCollection[6].Description = Resources.InterfacialVaporFrictionFactorRatio;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[6] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setFIFG;
				realProperty.Value = 0.0;
				realProperty.UBound = 10.0;
				realProperty.LBound = 0.0;
				lclRXTRPH = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclRXTRPH);
				propertyCollection[7].Description = Resources.PhaseChangeRelaxFactor;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[7] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setRXTRPH;
				realProperty.Value = 0.02;
				realProperty.UBound = 0.1;
				realProperty.LBound = 0.001;
				lclMAXVOL = new RealProperty();
				propertyCollection.Add(lclMAXVOL);
				propertyCollection[8].Description = Resources.MaxLiquidHoldup;
				realProperty = propertyCollection[8] as RealProperty;
				realProperty.AfterValueChanged += setMAXVOL;
				realProperty.Value = 0.995;
				realProperty.UBound = 1.0;
				realProperty.LBound = 0.9;
				enumValues = new EnumValues();
				num2 = 2;
				array = new string[num2];
				array[0] = Resources.HomogeneousLiquidPhase;
				array[1] = Resources.DiscreteLiquidPhase;
				array2 = new int[num2];
				array2[0] = 2;
				array2[1] = 3;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					enumValues.Add(array2[num], array[num]);
				}
				lclNPHASE = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 2);
				propertyCollection.Add(lclNPHASE);
				propertyCollection[9].Description = Resources.LiquidModel;
				enumProperty = propertyCollection[9] as EnumProperty;
				enumProperty.AfterValueChanged += setNPHASE;
				enumValues = new EnumValues();
				num2 = 2;
				array = new string[num2];
				array[0] = Resources.OffTxt;
				array[1] = Resources.CombinedPhase;
				array2 = new int[num2];
				array2[0] = 0;
				array2[1] = 1;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					enumValues.Add(array2[num], array[num]);
				}
				int value = 1;
				lclIENERGY = new EnumProperty(enumValues, value);
				propertyCollection.Add(lclIENERGY);
				propertyCollection[10].Description = Resources.HeatTransfer;
				enumProperty = propertyCollection[10] as EnumProperty;
				enumProperty.AfterValueChanged += setIENERGY;
				enumValues = new EnumValues();
				num2 = 2;
				array = new string[num2];
				array[0] = Resources.OffTxt;
				array[1] = Resources.OnTxt;
				array2 = new int[num2];
				array2[0] = 0;
				array2[1] = 1;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					enumValues.Add(array2[num], array[num]);
				}
				lclDISSIP = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 0);
				propertyCollection.Add(lclDISSIP);
				propertyCollection[11].Description = Resources.DissipationDuringHeatTransfer;
				enumProperty = propertyCollection[11] as EnumProperty;
				enumProperty.AfterValueChanged += setDISSIP;
				enumValues = new EnumValues();
				array = new string[num2];
				array[0] = Resources.OnTxt;
				array[1] = Resources.OffTxt;
				array2 = new int[2] { 1, 2 };
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					enumValues.Add(array2[num], array[num]);
				}
				lclTRANSI = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 2);
				propertyCollection.Add(lclTRANSI);
				propertyCollection[12].Description = Resources.CoolDown;
				enumProperty = propertyCollection[12] as EnumProperty;
				enumProperty.AfterValueChanged += setTransi;
				enumValues = new EnumValues();
				num2 = 4;
				array = new string[num2];
				array[0] = Resources.OffTxt;
				array[1] = Resources.HomogeneousFrozen;
				array[2] = Resources.HomogeneousEquilibrium;
				array[3] = Resources.HomogeneousFrozenSplit;
				array2 = new int[num2];
				array2[0] = 0;
				array2[1] = 1;
				array2[2] = 2;
				array2[3] = 4;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					enumValues.Add(array2[num], array[num]);
				}
				lclICFLOW = new EnumProperty(enumValues, 0);
				propertyCollection.Add(lclICFLOW);
				propertyCollection[13].Description = Resources.ChokeFlow;
				enumProperty = propertyCollection[13] as EnumProperty;
				enumProperty.AfterValueChanged += setICFLOW;
				enumValues = new EnumValues();
				num2 = 1;
				array[0] = Resources.InhibitedTxt;
				array2[0] = 0;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					enumValues.Add(array2[num], array[num]);
				}
				value = 0;
				lclIPHASE = new EnumProperty(enumValues, value);
				propertyCollection.Add(lclIPHASE);

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			int errCode = 0;
			AHD_DEALLOCATE(ref lclGSindex, ref errCode);
			if (errCode != 0)
			{
				throw new Exception(Resources.FailedToDeallocateGlobalStore1);
			}
			AHD_DESTRUCTOR(ref errCode);
			if (errCode != 0)
			{
				throw new Exception(Resources.FailedToDeallocateGlobalStore2);
			}
			if (!mDisposed)
			{
				mDisposed = true;
			}
		}

		~CompositionSolver()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		public override void setNetworkDirty()
		{
			int errCode = 0;
			UnitOpSolver unitOpSolver = null;
			base.NetworkDirty = true;
			int count = Network.UnitOps.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				unitOpSolver = Network.UnitOps[i].SolverUnitOp;
				unitOpSolver.UnitOpDirty = true;
			}
			AHD_RESET(ref lclGSindex, ref errCode);
			if (errCode != 0)
			{
				throw new Exception(Resources.GlobalStoreIndexOutOfBounds);
			}
		}

		private void setMXAUTO(object e, double i)
		{
			IntegerProperty integerProperty = e as IntegerProperty;
			NumberOfAutomaticRecovery = integerProperty.Value;
		}

		private void setDTMAX(object e, double i)
		{
			RealProperty realProperty = e as RealProperty;
			double num = realProperty.Value;
			if (realProperty.Value > 100.0)
			{
				num = 100.0;
			}
			if (num < 1E-05)
			{
				num = 1E-05;
			}
			if (realProperty.Value < LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDTMIN)
			{
				num = LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDTMIN;
			}
			LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDTMAX = num;
			if (LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDelt > num)
			{
				LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDelt = num;
			}
			if (lclDelt.Value > num)
			{
				lclDelt.Value = num;
			}
			realProperty.Value = num;
		}

		private void setDTMIN(object e, double i)
		{
			RealProperty realProperty = e as RealProperty;
			double num = realProperty.Value;
			if (num > 10.0)
			{
				num = 10.0;
			}
			if (num < 1E-06)
			{
				num = 1E-06;
			}
			if (num > LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDTMAX)
			{
				num = LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDTMAX;
			}
			LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDTMIN = num;
			realProperty.Value = num;
			if (LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDelt < realProperty.Value)
			{
				LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclDelt = realProperty.Value;
			}
			if (lclDelt.Value < realProperty.Value)
			{
				lclDelt.Value = realProperty.Value;
			}
		}

		private static void SetEnvironmentVariables()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			Version version = typeof(DynamicSolver).Assembly.GetName().Version;
			string text3 = version.Major + "." + version.Minor;
			registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\AOS\\" + text3);
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				text = registryKey.GetValue("AOSTOP").ToString() + "\\bin";
			}
			registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\AMSystem\\" + text3 + "\\Dir");
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				text = text + ";" + registryKey.GetValue("bin").ToString();
			}
			registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\APrSystem\\" + text3 + "\\aplus");
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				text = text + ";" + registryKey.GetValue("asptop").ToString() + "\\xeq";
			}
			registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aspen Properties\\" + text3 + "\\aplus");
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				text = text + ";" + registryKey.GetValue("asptop").ToString() + "\\xeq";
			}
			registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\OOMF\\" + text3);
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				text = text + ";" + registryKey.GetValue("OOMFTOP").ToString() + "\\xeq";
			}
			if (text.Length > 0)
			{
				text2 = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH");
				text2 = text + ";" + text2;
				Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", text2);
			}
		}

		public void ColdStartMoleFraction(int iComp, double molefraction)
		{
			if (mColdStartMoleFractions == null)
			{
				mColdStartMoleFractions = new FractionList();
			}
			while (iComp >= mColdStartMoleFractions.Count)
			{
				mColdStartMoleFractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(0.0));
			}
			mColdStartMoleFractions[iComp].Value = molefraction;
		}

		public override void InletOptions(ref PortDirectionEnum[] inlets)
		{
			inlets = new PortDirectionEnum[1];
			inlets[0] = PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity;
		}

		public override void OutletOptions(ref PortDirectionEnum[] outlets)
		{
			outlets = new PortDirectionEnum[1];
			outlets[0] = PortDirectionEnum.PortPressure;
		}

		private unsafe void PlacAllocation(int count)
		{
			count += 2;
			int i = 0;
			lclPlac = new FPlac[count];
			lclMDO = new FortranMDO[count];
			MessageLogger iMessages = new MessageLogger();
			base.IMessages = iMessages;
			GClclPlac = GCHandle.Alloc(lclPlac, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			GClclMDO = GCHandle.Alloc(lclMDO, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			for (; i < count; i++)
			{
				lclPlac[i].Initialise();
				lclMDO[i].ModelIndex = 600;
				lclMDO[i].status = 0;
				lclMDO[i].ModelAddress = GClclPlac.AddrOfPinnedObject() + sizeof(FPlac) * i;
				lclMDO[i].IntegrationAddress = lclAddressIntegrationFunctionFortran;
			}
			IsSet = false;
		}

		internal void PlacDeallocation()
		{
			if (GClclMDO.IsAllocated)
			{
				GClclMDO.Free();
			}
			if (GClclPlac.IsAllocated)
			{
				GClclPlac.Free();
			}
		}

		internal int SolveInFortran(IntPtr PLAdd)
		{
			try
			{
				return PPLINTEGRATEPLAC(PLAdd.ToInt64(), base.IMessages, null, null, ref lclGSindex);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void FSolve()
		{
			int num = 0;
			try
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				int phasesSupported = base.PhasesSupported;
				bool flag = base.IsInitialised;
				if (!flag || IDIndex == null || MWIndex == null)

		private void GetActiveUnitOps(IUnitOpCollection theUnitOps)
		{
			if (!base.IsInitialised)
			{
				for (int num = mActiveUnitOps.Count; num > 0; num--)
				{
					mActiveUnitOps.DeleteWithIndex(num - 1);
				}
			}
			if (mActiveUnitOps.Count != 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			ObjectAnalysisStatus[] unitOperationStatus = GetUnitOperationStatus(theUnitOps);
			for (int i = 0; i < theUnitOps.Count; i++)
			{
				UnitOp unitOp = theUnitOps[i];
				if (!unitOperationStatus[i].Excluded && unitOperationStatus[i].SeePressure)
				{
					unitOp.ExcludedBySolver = false;
					mActiveUnitOps.add(unitOp);
				}
				else
				{
					unitOp.ExcludedBySolver = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void Solve()
		{
			string message = "";
			GetActiveUnitOps(mNetwork.UnitOps);
			if (!IsSet)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < mActiveUnitOps.Count; i++)
				{
					(mActiveUnitOps[i].SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).SetComputationalDomain();
				}
				RecreateCompDomain = false;
				IsSet = false;
			}
			int num = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < mActiveUnitOps.Count; j++)
			{
				num += (mActiveUnitOps[j].SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).PlacCount;
			}
			PlacAllocation(num);
			if (IsValid(ref message))
			{
				if (!base.IsInitialised)
				{
					lclDelt.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				SetPlacArrayMDO();
				FSolve();
				PlacDeallocation();
				timeStepCount = ++timeStepCount;
				if (timeStepCount == 50)
				{
					GC.Collect();
					timeStepCount = 0L;
				}
				return;
			}
			PlacDeallocation();
			GC.Collect();
			timeStepCount = 0L;
			(solverStatus[0] as StringProperty).Value = message;
			throw new Exception(message);
		}

		public override bool IsValid(ref string message)
		{
			try
			{
				message = "";
				bool flag = true;
				int num = 0;
				while (flag && num < mActiveUnitOps.Count)
				{
					flag = mActiveUnitOps[num].IsValidDyn(ref message);
					num++;
				}
				return flag;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "IsValid"), innerException);
			}
		}

		public override bool IsSolvable(ref string message)
		{
			try
			{
				if (mIMessages != null)
				{
					mIMessages.Clear();
				}
				if (message == "NO_DISPLAY")
				{
					CalcDisplay = false;
				}
				else
				{
					CalcDisplay = true;
				}
				message = "";
				GetActiveUnitOps(mNetwork.UnitOps);
				if (!base.IsInitialised)
				{
					setNetworkDirty();
				}
				bool flag = true;
				IHydraulicPhase[] array = null;
				int num = 0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				int i = 0;
				int num4 = 0;
				Property item = base.SolverSpecificProperties.GetItem(25);
				Property item2 = base.SolverSpecificProperties.GetItem(26);
				Property item3 = base.SolverSpecificProperties.GetItem(27);
				Property item4 = base.SolverSpecificProperties.GetItem(28);
				for (int j = 0; j < mActiveUnitOps.Count; j++)
				{
					if (mActiveUnitOps[j].SolverUnitOp is PlacMultiSegPipe)
					{
						(mActiveUnitOps[j].SolverUnitOp as PlacMultiSegPipe).InitialisePipeSegments();
					}
				}
				int num5 = 0;
				for (int k = 0; k < mActiveUnitOps.Count; k++)
				{
					int count = mActiveUnitOps[k].PortCollection.Count;
					for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
					{
						AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port = mActiveUnitOps[k].PortCollection.Item(l);
						if (port.PortType == PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity)
						{
							IFractionList componentMoleFractions = port.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions;
							num5 = Math.Max(num5, componentMoleFractions.Count);
							if (num5 <= 0)
							{
								return false;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNCPDS == -32767)
				{
					LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNCPDS = num5;
				}
				if (!base.IsInitialised && DynamicInitialization == DynInitializationMethod.ColdStart && mColdStartMoleFractions != null && !mColdStartMoleFractions.HasEmpty())
				{
					for (int m = 0; m < mActiveUnitOps.Count; m++)
					{
						UnitOp unitOp = mActiveUnitOps[m];
						int num6 = unitOp.PointValues.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
						for (int n = 0; n < num6; n++)
						{
							HydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = unitOp.PointValues[n].FlowStream as HydraulicFlowStream;
							for (int num7 = 0; num7 < LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNCPDS; num7++)
							{
								hydraulicFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions[num7].Value = mColdStartMoleFractions[num7].Value;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					flag = CheckBoundary(item, item3, item4, item2);
				}
				if (flag && (!base.IsInitialised || CalcDisplay || base.NetworkDirty))
				{
					if (base.NetworkDirty)
					{
						base.NetworkDirty = false;
					}
					flag = CheckPointValues(item, item3, item4, item2);
				}
				for (; i < mActiveUnitOps.Count; i++)
				{
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < mActiveUnitOps[i].PortCollection.Count; num4++)
					{
						flag = true;
						if (!mActiveUnitOps[i].IsPortActive(num4))
						{
							continue;
						}
						num = mActiveUnitOps[i].PortCollection.Item(num4).FlowStream.NumberOfPhases + 1;
						IHydraulicPhase[] array2 = new HydraulicPhase[num];
						array = array2;
						for (int num8 = 0; num8 < num; num8++)
						{
							array[num8] = mActiveUnitOps[i].PortCollection.Item(num4).FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(num8) as IHydraulicPhase;
						}
						num3 = mActiveUnitOps[i].PortCollection.Item(num4).Diameter.Value;
						num2 = num3 * num3 * Math.PI * 0.25;
						for (int num9 = 0; num9 < num; num9++)
						{
							double value = array[num9].PerCentAreaOccupied.Value;
							double value2 = array[num9].MassFlowRate.Value;
							double value3 = array[num9].Density.Value;
							if (value2 == 0.0 || value < 1E-06 || value3 * num2 * value < 1E-09)
							{
								array[num9].Velocity.Value = 0.0;
							}
							else
							{
								array[num9].Velocity.Value = value2 / (value3 * num2 * value);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					flag = CheckNumberOfPhases();
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					CheckNetworkLinkage();
					flag = base.IsSolvable(ref message);
				}
				return flag;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				message = string.Format(Resources.MethodFailedWithException, "DynamicSolver.IsSolvable") + innerException.Message;
				while (innerException.InnerException != null)
				{
					innerException = innerException.InnerException;
					message = message + " " + innerException.Message;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private int GetFortranComponentID(UnitOp thisUnitOp)
		{
			int eMPTY = IntegerProperty.EMPTY;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (lclPlac == null)
			{
				return eMPTY;
			}
			int count = mActiveUnitOps.Count;
			int num3 = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			while (!flag && num3 < count)
			{
				num = (mActiveUnitOps[num3].SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).PlacCount;
				num2 += num;
				if (mActiveUnitOps[num3] is MultiSegPipe)
				{
					MultiSegPipe multiSegPipe = mActiveUnitOps[num3] as MultiSegPipe;
					int numberOfSegments = multiSegPipe.NumberOfSegments;
					PipeSegment pipeSegment = null;
					int num4 = 0;
					for (int i = 0; i < numberOfSegments; i++)
					{
						pipeSegment = multiSegPipe.GetPipeSegment(i) as PipeSegment;
						if (pipeSegment.Name == thisUnitOp.Name)
						{
							int num5 = num2 - num + num4 + 1;
							eMPTY = lclPlac[num5].lclComponent_Number;
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
						num4 += (pipeSegment.SolverUnitOp as PlacPipeSegment).PlacCount;
					}
				}
				else if (mActiveUnitOps[num3].Name == thisUnitOp.Name)
				{
					eMPTY = lclPlac[num2 - num + 1].lclComponent_Number;
					flag = true;
				}
				num3++;
			}
			return eMPTY;
		}

		private string GetNameFromComponentID(int fortranID)

			{
				num = (mActiveUnitOps[num3].SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac).PlacCount;
				num2 += num;
				if (mActiveUnitOps[num3] is MultiSegPipe)
				{
					MultiSegPipe multiSegPipe = mActiveUnitOps[num3] as MultiSegPipe;
					int numberOfSegments = multiSegPipe.NumberOfSegments;
					PipeSegment pipeSegment = null;

					{

						int num5 = num2 - num + num4 + 1;
						int num6 = lclPlac[num5].lclComponent_Number;
						if (fortranID == num6)
						{
							result = pipeSegment.Name;
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
						num4 += (pipeSegment.SolverUnitOp as PlacPipeSegment).PlacCount;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int num7 = lclPlac[num2 - num + 1].lclComponent_Number;
					if (fortranID == num7)
					{
						result = mActiveUnitOps[num3].Name;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				num3++;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool CopyComposition()
		{
			IFractionList fractionList = null;
			int num = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			int num3 = -1;
			int num4 = -1;
			int num5 = -1;
			int num6 = -1;
			int num7 = -1;
			int num8 = -1;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.IPort port = null;
			bool result = true;
			IPhysicalPropertyPackage physicalPropertyPackage = null;
			FlashCalculationLevelEnum flashCalculationLevelEnum = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.Thermal;
			HydraulicFlowStream hydraulicFlowStream = null;
			try
			{
				num7 = 0;
				num5 = base.Network.Count;
				for (num = 0; num < num5; num++)
				{
					num6 = base.Network.UnitOps[num].PortCollection.Count;
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < num6; num4++)
					{
						port = base.Network.UnitOps[num].PortCollection.Item(num4);
						if (port.PortType == PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity)
						{
							fractionList = port.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions;
							num7 = Math.Max(num7, fractionList.Count);
							if (num7 <= 0)
							{
								return false;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNCPDS == -32767)
				{
					LocalCopyOfPlacData.lclNCPDS = num7;
				}
				int phasesSupported = ((ISolver)this).PhasesSupported;
				int num9 = phasesSupported;
				for (num = 0; num < num5; num++)
				{
					num6 = base.Network.UnitOps[num].PortCollection.Count;
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < num6; num4++)
					{
						port = base.Network.UnitOps[num].PortCollection.Item(num4);
						if (port.PortType == PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity)
						{
							continue;
						}
						num8 = port.FlowStream.NumberOfPhases;
						num9 = Math.Min(num9, num8);
						for (num2 = 0; num2 <= num8; num2++)
						{
							phase = port.FlowStream.GetPhaseByID(num2);
							if (phase.ComponentMoleFractions == null || phase.ComponentMoleFractions.Count != num7)
							{

								else

									for (num3 = 0; num3 < num7; num3++)

									}
								}
							}
							else
							{
								if (phase.PhaseType != PhaseType.Overall)
								{
									continue;
								}
								for (num3 = 0; num3 < num7; num3++)
								{
									if (phase.ComponentMoleFractions[num3].Value == RealValue.FEMPTY)
									{
										phase.ComponentMoleFractions[num3].Value = fractionList[num3].Value;
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					num6 = base.Network.UnitOps[num].PointValues.GetUpperBound(0);
					for (num4 = 0; num4 <= num6; num4++)

								}

									{

								}

								flowStream.MassFlowrate.Value = value;
							}
							else if (phasesSupported == 3 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{
								flowStream.NumberOfPhases = phasesSupported;
								if (phase2.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
								{

									flowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(phasesSupported, phase2);
									flowStream.GetPhaseByID(num8).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								}
							}
							else
							{

								}
								else if (physicalPropertyPackage is ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo)
								{

									(physicalPropertyPackage as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermo).CalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid;
								}

								physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.ThermalAndTransport;

								{

								}

								physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = flashCalculationLevelEnum;
								phase2 = flowStream.GetPhaseByID(num8).Clone() as IPhase;

										break;

								}
								flowStream.NumberOfPhases = phasesSupported;
								if (num10 > 0.25)
								{
									flowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(num8, phase2);
									phase2.PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
									flowStream.SetPhaseValueByID(phasesSupported, phase2);

									flowStream.GetPhaseByID(phasesSupported).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;

							num8 = flowStream3.NumberOfPhases;
							if (num8 != phasesSupported - 1)
							{

							}

							if (phasesSupported == 2 && flowStream3.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{

								phase2.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;

								flowStream3.MassFlowrate.Value = value2;
							}
							else if (phasesSupported == 3 && flowStream3.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid) == null)
							{

								{

									flowStream3.GetPhaseByID(num8).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
									flowStream3.SetPhaseValueByID(phasesSupported, phase2);
									flowStream3.GetPhaseByID(num8).MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
								}
							}
							else
							{

								{

								hydraulicFlowStream.UpdateStreamByValue(flowStream4);
								physicalPropertyPackage.FlashLevel = flashCalculationLevelEnum;

								for (num3 = 0; num3 < num7; num3++)

						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "DynamicSolver.CopyComposition"), innerException);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool CheckBoundary(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha)
		{
			try
			{
				bool flag = true;
				bool flag2 = base.IsInitialised;
				double value = (lclRelaxFactor as IRealProperty).Value;
				int phasesSupported = ((ISolver)this).PhasesSupported;
				int i = 0;
				int count = mActiveUnitOps.Count;
				UnitOpPlac unitOpPlac = null;
				if (!flag2 || oldPressure == null)
				{
					oldPressure = null;
					oldPressure = new double[count];
				}
				for (; i < count; i++)
				{
					UnitOp unitOp = mActiveUnitOps[i];
					if (unitOp.SolverUnitOp is UnitOpPlac)
					{
						unitOpPlac = unitOp.SolverUnitOp as UnitOpPlac;
						unitOp.PropertyPackage = mThermo;
						flag = unitOpPlac.CheckBoundary(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, flag2, CalcDisplay, phasesSupported, oldPressure[i], value);
						if (!flag)
						{
							i = count;
						}
					}
				}
				return flag;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "DynamicSolver.CheckBoundary"), innerException);
			}
		}

		private bool CheckPointValues(Property prop, Property cspres, Property cstemp, Property fxalpha)
		{
			try
			{
				bool flag = true;
				bool isInit = base.IsInitialised;
				int i = 0;
				int num = 0;
				IUnitOpPlac unitOpPlac = null;
				for (num = mActiveUnitOps.Count; i < num; i++)
				{
					UnitOp unitOp = mActiveUnitOps[i];
					if (unitOp.SolverUnitOp is IUnitOpPlac)
					{
						unitOpPlac = unitOp.SolverUnitOp as IUnitOpPlac;
						if (unitOp.PropertyPackage == null)
						{
							unitOp.PropertyPackage = mThermo;
						}
						flag = unitOpPlac.CheckPointValues(prop, cspres, cstemp, fxalpha, isInit);
						if (!flag)
						{
							i = num;
						}
					}
				}
				return flag;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "DynamicSolver.CheckPointValues"), innerException);
			}
		}

namespace AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework

	[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 8)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal struct FBoundaryCondition
	{
		internal unsafe AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid* MyFluid;

		internal int dirty;

		private int Status;

		internal int VelocityBC;

		internal int PressureBC;

		internal FortranMDO MyMDO;

		internal bool Dirty
		{
			get
			{
				if (dirty == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!value)
				{
					dirty = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					dirty = 1;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InitFBoundaryCondition()
		{
			dirty = 1;
			Status = 0;
			VelocityBC = 2;
			PressureBC = 2;
		}

		internal unsafe void associateFluid(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid* RefMyFLuid)
		{
			MyFluid = RefMyFLuid;
		}

		internal unsafe void NullifyBC()
		{
			if (MyFluid != null)
			{
				MyFluid->NullifyFluid();
				MyFluid = null;
			}
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal class CBoundaryCondition
	{
		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid MycSharpFluid;

		internal IHydraulicFlowStream mFlowStream;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FBoundaryCondition MyFBoundaryCondition;

		internal GCHandle[] gcHandleBC;

		internal bool Dirty
		{
			get
			{
				return MyFBoundaryCondition.Dirty;
			}
			set
			{
				MyFBoundaryCondition.Dirty = value;
			}
		}

		internal CBoundaryCondition()
		{
			mFlowStream = new HydraulicFlowStream();
			MyFBoundaryCondition = default(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FBoundaryCondition);
			MyFBoundaryCondition.InitFBoundaryCondition();
			gcHandleBC = new GCHandle[1];
		}

		internal void CopyStreamFromFStreamBC(double area, bool velBC, double[] LastCellHoldUp)
		{
			int numberOfPhases = mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases;
			int i = 0;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			for (; i < MycSharpFluid.NPhases && i <= LastCellHoldUp.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp = LastCellHoldUp[i];
				num += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp;
			}
			int num3 = 0;
			i = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase;
			if (numberOfPhases > 1)
			{
				for (; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					hydraulicPhase = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1) as HydraulicPhase;
					hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cp;
					hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cv;
					hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density;
					hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Enthalpy;
					hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].InternalEnergy;
					hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp / num;
					hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Pressure;
					hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Temperature;
					hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity;
					hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Viscosity;
					hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].DF_DP;
					for (num3 = 0; num3 < MycSharpFluid.nc; num3++)
					{
						hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num3].Value = MycSharpFluid.GetComponentMassFractions(i + 1, num3);
					}
					if (!velBC)
					{
						hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * LastCellHoldUp[i] * area;
						num2 += hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value;
					}
					hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
					hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
				}
			}
			if (numberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1) as HydraulicPhase;
				if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					num4 = 0;
				}
				else if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
				{
					num4 = 1;
				}
				else if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
				{
					num4 = 2;
				}
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Cp;
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Cv;
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Density;
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Enthalpy;
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].InternalEnergy;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Pressure;
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Temperature;
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Viscosity;
				hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].DF_DP;
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].Velocity;
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num4].mHoldUp / num;
				for (num3 = 0; num3 < MycSharpFluid.nc; num3++)
				{
					hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num3].Value = MycSharpFluid.GetComponentMassFractions(num4 + 1, num3);
				}
				if (!velBC)
				{
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp * area;
					num2 += hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value;
				}
				hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
				i++;
			}
			hydraulicPhase = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0) as HydraulicPhase;
			for (num3 = 0; num3 < MycSharpFluid.nc; num3++)
			{
				hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num3].Value = MycSharpFluid.GetComponentMassFractions(0, num3);
			}
			hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
			hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
			if (numberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Cp;
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Cv;
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Density;
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Enthalpy;
				hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].FlowRate;
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].InternalEnergy;
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Pressure;
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Temperature;
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Velocity;
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Viscosity;
				hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].DF_DP;
				if (!velBC)
				{
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Density * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp * area / num;
				}
				else
				{
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = (mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(1) as HydraulicPhase).MassFlowRate.Value;
				}
			}
			else if (numberOfPhases > 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Pressure;
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Temperature;
				hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].DF_DP;
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = 0.0;
				int num5 = Math.Min(numberOfPhases, MycSharpFluid.MyPhases.GetUpperBound(0) + 1);
				for (i = 0; i < num5; i++)
				{
					double num6 = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp / num;
					hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cp * num6;
					hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cv * num6;
					hydraulicPhase.Density.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * num6;
					hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Enthalpy * num6;
					hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].InternalEnergy * num6;
					hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * num6;
					hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Viscosity * num6;
				}
				if (!velBC)
				{
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = num2;
				}
				else
				{
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = 0.0;
					for (i = 1; i <= numberOfPhases; i++)
					{
						hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value += (mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i) as HydraulicPhase).MassFlowRate.Value;
					}
				}
			}
			if (numberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
				{
					hydraulicPhase = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i) as HydraulicPhase;
					hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
				}
				return;
			}
			double num7 = 0.0;
			for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
			{
				num7 += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].EqMassFraction;
			}
			double[] array = new double[numberOfPhases];
			double[] array2 = new double[numberOfPhases];
			double[] array3 = new double[numberOfPhases];
			double num8 = 0.0;
			for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
			{
				array[i] = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].EqMassFraction / num7;
				array2[i] = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MolecularWeight.Value;
				array3[i] = array[i] / array2[i];
				num8 += array3[i];
			}
			double[] array4 = new double[numberOfPhases];
			for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
			{
				array4[i] = array3[i] / num8;
			}
			mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
			mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
			for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
			{
				mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MolarVapourFraction.Value = array4[i];
				mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MolarFraction.Value = array4[i];
			}
		}

		internal void InstanciateFluidFortran(int NPhases)
		{
			if (MycSharpFluid == null)
			{
				MycSharpFluid = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid(NPhases);
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void SetFluidForFortranBC()
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid* ptr = MycSharpFluid.ReturnNestedFluid();
			MycSharpFluid.GCAlloc();
			ptr->SetPhasePointer(MycSharpFluid.PhaseHandle.AddrOfPinnedObject());
			if (gcHandleBC[0].IsAllocated)
			{
				gcHandleBC[0].Free();
			}
			gcHandleBC[0] = GCHandle.Alloc(*ptr, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			MyFBoundaryCondition.associateFluid((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid*)(void*)gcHandleBC[0].AddrOfPinnedObject());
		}

		internal unsafe void nullifyFluid()
		{
			GCFree();
			MyFBoundaryCondition.associateFluid(null);
		}

		internal void GCFree()
		{
			if (gcHandleBC[0].IsAllocated)
			{
				gcHandleBC[0].Free();
			}
			MycSharpFluid.GCFree();
		}

		internal void GetFluid(ref IFlowStream RefFlowStream)
		{
			RefFlowStream = mFlowStream;
		}

		internal void UndefinedVBC()
		{
			MyFBoundaryCondition.VelocityBC = 2;
		}

		internal void UndefinedPBC()
		{
			MyFBoundaryCondition.PressureBC = 2;
		}

		internal void SetImposedSupvelBC(double[] FlowRate)
		{
			VelocityBC();
			int numberOfPhases = mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases;
			IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase;
			for (int i = 0; i < numberOfPhases + 1; i++)
			{
				hydraulicPhase = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i) as HydraulicPhase;
				hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = FlowRate[i];
			}
			hydraulicPhase = null;
		}

		internal void PressureBC(ref bool flag)
		{
			if (MyFBoundaryCondition.PressureBC == 0)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			else
			{
				flag = false;
			}
		}

		internal void PressureBC()
		{
			MyFBoundaryCondition.PressureBC = 0;
			MyFBoundaryCondition.VelocityBC = 1;
		}

		internal void VelPresBC()
		{
			MyFBoundaryCondition.PressureBC = 0;
			MyFBoundaryCondition.VelocityBC = 0;
		}

		internal void VelocityBC(ref bool flag)
		{
			if (MyFBoundaryCondition.VelocityBC == 0)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			else
			{
				flag = false;
			}
		}

		internal void VelocityBC()
		{
			MyFBoundaryCondition.VelocityBC = 0;
			MyFBoundaryCondition.PressureBC = 1;
		}

		internal void SetPressureBC(double[] Pressures)
		{
			PressureBC();
			int numberOfPhases = mFlowStream.NumberOfPhases;
			IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase;
			for (int i = 0; i < numberOfPhases + 1; i++)
			{
				hydraulicPhase = mFlowStream.GetPhaseByID(i) as HydraulicPhase;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = Pressures[i];
			}
			hydraulicPhase = null;
		}

		internal void SetFBCType(IPort port)
		{
			switch (port.PortType)
			{
			case PortDirectionEnum.PortPressure:
				PressureBC();
				break;
			case PortDirectionEnum.PortVelocity:
				VelocityBC();
				break;
			case PortDirectionEnum.PortUndefined:
				UndefinedPBC();
				UndefinedVBC();
				break;
			case PortDirectionEnum.PortVelPres:
				VelPresBC();
				break;
			case PortDirectionEnum.PortDefined:
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 8)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal class FCHM
	{
		[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 8)]
		[ComVisible(false)]
		internal struct Connectivity
		{
			internal IntPtr RightCHM;

			internal IntPtr LeftCHM;

			internal IntPtr SideCHM;

			internal void NullifyConnectivity()
			{
				RightCHM = IntPtr.Zero;
				LeftCHM = IntPtr.Zero;
				SideCHM = IntPtr.Zero;
			}
		}

		internal Connectivity MyConnectivity;

		internal unsafe AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary* MyUnitOPSummary;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid MyFluid;

		internal unsafe AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FBoundaryCondition* MyFBoundaryCondition;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FGeom MyFGeom;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulation MyFInsulation;

		internal int endofgrid;

		internal int beginningofgrid;

		private int Index;

		private int Status;

		internal int MyPPPIndex;

		internal FortranMDO MyMDO;

		internal bool EndOfGrid
		{
			get
			{
				if (endofgrid == 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!value)
				{
					endofgrid = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					endofgrid = 1;
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool BeginninOfGrid
		{
			get
			{
				if (beginningofgrid == 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!value)
				{
					beginningofgrid = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					beginningofgrid = 1;
				}
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void NullifyCHM()
		{
			MyConnectivity.NullifyConnectivity();
			if (MyUnitOPSummary != null)
			{
				MyUnitOPSummary->NullifyUnitOpSummary();
				MyUnitOPSummary = null;
			}
			MyFluid.NullifyFluid();
			if (MyFBoundaryCondition != null)
			{
				MyFBoundaryCondition->NullifyBC();
				MyFBoundaryCondition = null;
			}
			MyFGeom.NullifyFGeomType();
			MyFInsulation.NullifyFInsulation();
		}

		internal void InitFCHM()
		{
		}

		internal void SetIndex(int i)
		{
			Index = i;
		}

		internal unsafe void AssociateBC(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FBoundaryCondition* refMyFBoundaryCondition)
		{
			MyFBoundaryCondition = refMyFBoundaryCondition;
		}

		internal void AssociateFluid(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid RefToFluid)
		{
			MyFluid = RefToFluid;
		}

		internal void AssociateSideCHM(IntPtr RefSideCHM)
		{
			MyConnectivity.SideCHM = RefSideCHM;
		}

		internal void AssociateLeftCHM(IntPtr RefLeftCHM)
		{
			MyConnectivity.LeftCHM = RefLeftCHM;
		}

		internal void AssociateRightCHM(IntPtr RefRightCHM)
		{
			MyConnectivity.RightCHM = RefRightCHM;
		}

		internal void AssociateInsulaiton(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulation RefFInsulation)
		{
			MyFInsulation = RefFInsulation;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal class CConnectivity
	{
		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM MyRightCCHM;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM MyLeftCCHM;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM MySideCCHM;
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal class CCHM
	{
		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCHM MyFCHM;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CConnectivity MyCConnectivity;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation MyInsulation;

		internal IFlowStream mStream;

		internal IInsulation mInsulation;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid MycSharpFluid;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CompDomain MyCompDomain;

		internal CCHM()
		{
			MyFCHM = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCHM();
			MyFCHM.InitFCHM();
			MyCConnectivity = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CConnectivity();
			MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM = null;
			MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM = null;
			MyCConnectivity.MySideCCHM = null;
			MyCompDomain = null;
		}

		internal void CHMXMLStore(XmlTextWriter tw, int i)
		{
			int num = i;
			tw.WriteStartElement("Element");
			tw.WriteAttributeString("Number", num.ToString());
			tw.WriteStartElement("Fluid");
			if (mStream != null)
			{
				tw.WriteAttributeString("NumberOfPhases", mStream.NumberOfPhases.ToString());
				IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase = null;
				for (int j = 0; j < mStream.NumberOfPhases + 1; j++)
				{
					tw.WriteStartElement("Phase");
					hydraulicPhase = mStream.GetPhaseByID(j) as IHydraulicPhase;
					tw.WriteAttributeString("PhaseType", hydraulicPhase.PhaseType.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Conductivity", hydraulicPhase.Conductivity.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Cp", hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("CriticalPressure", hydraulicPhase.CriticalPressure.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Cv", hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Density", hydraulicPhase.Density.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Enthalpy", hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Entropy", hydraulicPhase.Entropy.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("InternalEnergy", hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("MassFraction", hydraulicPhase.MassFraction.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("MolarFraction", hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("MolecularWeight", hydraulicPhase.MolecularWeight.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("MolarFraction", hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("NumberOfCompounds", hydraulicPhase.NumberOfCompounds.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("PerCentAreaOccupied", hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Pressure", hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("SurfaceTension", hydraulicPhase.SurfaceTension.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Temperature", hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteElementString("Viscosity", hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value.ToString());
					tw.WriteEndElement();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				tw.WriteAttributeString("Fluid", "NoFLuidPresent");
			}
			tw.WriteEndElement();
			if (MyInsulation != null)
			{
				MyInsulation.InsulationXMLStore(tw);
			}
			else
			{
				tw.WriteStartElement("Insulation");
				tw.WriteAttributeString("Insulation", "NoInsulationPresent");
				tw.WriteEndElement();
			}
			tw.WriteEndElement();
		}

		internal void CopyStreamFromFStream(ref IFlowStream mStream, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid MycSharpFluid, double Area)
		{
			int numberOfPhases = mStream.NumberOfPhases;
			IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase = null;
			int i = 0;
			double num = 0.0;
			for (; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
			{
				num += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			i = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			if (numberOfPhases > 1)
			{
				for (; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					hydraulicPhase = mStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1) as IHydraulicPhase;
					hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cp;
					hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cv;
					hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density;
					hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Enthalpy;
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].FlowRate;
					hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].InternalEnergy;
					hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp / num;
					hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Pressure;
					hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Temperature;
					hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity;
					hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Viscosity;
					hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].DF_DP;
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp * Area / num;
					for (num2 = 0; num2 < MycSharpFluid.nc; num2++)
					{
						hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num2].Value = MycSharpFluid.GetComponentMassFractions(i + 1, num2);
					}
				}
			}
			if (numberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase = mStream.GetPhaseByID(1) as IHydraulicPhase;
				if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					num3 = 0;
				}
				else if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
				{
					num3 = 1;
				}
				else if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
				{
					num3 = 2;
				}
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Cp;
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Cv;
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Density;
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Enthalpy;
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].InternalEnergy;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Pressure;
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Temperature;
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Viscosity;
				hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].DF_DP;
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].Velocity;
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[num3].mHoldUp / num;
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < MycSharpFluid.nc; num2++)
				{
					hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num2].Value = MycSharpFluid.GetComponentMassFractions(num3 + 1, num2);
				}
				hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp * Area / num;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].mHoldUp / num;
				i++;
			}
			hydraulicPhase = mStream.GetPhaseByID(0) as IHydraulicPhase;
			for (num2 = 0; num2 < MycSharpFluid.nc; num2++)
			{
				hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num2].Value = MycSharpFluid.GetComponentMassFractions(0, num2);
			}
			if (numberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Cp;
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Cv;
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Density;
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Enthalpy;
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].InternalEnergy;
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Pressure;
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Temperature;
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Velocity;
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Viscosity;
				hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].DF_DP;
				hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Density * Area;
			}
			else if (numberOfPhases > 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Pressure;
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].Temperature;
				hydraulicPhase.DF_DP.Value = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[0].DF_DP;
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = 0.0;
				hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = 0.0;
				int num4 = Math.Min(numberOfPhases, MycSharpFluid.MyPhases.GetUpperBound(0) + 1);
				for (i = 0; i < num4; i++)
				{
					double num5 = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].mHoldUp / num;
					hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cp * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Cv * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.Density.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Enthalpy * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].InternalEnergy * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Viscosity * num5;
					hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Velocity * MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].Density * num5 * Area;
				}
			}
			if (numberOfPhases == 1)
			{
				hydraulicPhase = mStream.GetPhaseByID(0) as IHydraulicPhase;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase = mStream.GetPhaseByID(1) as IHydraulicPhase;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
				hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else if (numberOfPhases > 1)
			{
				double num6 = 0.0;
				for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					num6 += MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].EqMassFraction;
				}
				double[] array = new double[numberOfPhases];
				double[] array2 = new double[numberOfPhases];
				double[] array3 = new double[numberOfPhases];
				double num7 = 0.0;
				for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					array[i] = MycSharpFluid.MyPhases[i].EqMassFraction / num6;
					array2[i] = mStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MolecularWeight.Value;
					array3[i] = array[i] / array2[i];
					num7 += array3[i];
				}
				double[] array4 = new double[numberOfPhases];
				for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					array4[i] = array3[i] / num7;
				}
				mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MassFraction.Value = 1.0;
				mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
				mStream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
				for (i = 0; i < numberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					mStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MassFraction.Value = array[i];
					mStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MolarVapourFraction.Value = array4[i];
					mStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).MolarFraction.Value = array4[i];
				}
			}
		}

		internal void CopyInsulationFromInsulation(ref IInsulation insulation, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation StructInsulation)
		{
			int nLayers = insulation.NLayers;
			int i = 0;
			double Val = 0.0;
			for (; i < nLayers; i++)
			{
				StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Conductivity, i, ref Val);
				insulation.InsulationLayers[i].Conductivity.Value = Val;
				StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.c, i, ref Val);
				insulation.InsulationLayers[i].SpecificHeat.Value = Val;
				StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Density, i, ref Val);
				insulation.InsulationLayers[i].Density.Value = Val;
				StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Heat_Source, i, ref Val);
				insulation.InsulationLayers[i].HeatSource.Value = Val;
				StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Temperature, i, ref Val);
				insulation.InsulationLayers[i].InternalTemperature.Value = Val;
				if (i == nLayers - 1)
				{
					StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.ExtTemperature, i, ref Val);
					insulation.InsulationLayers[i].ExternalTemperature.Value = Val;
				}
				StructInsulation.GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Thickness, i, ref Val);
				insulation.InsulationLayers[i].Thickness.Value = Val;
			}
		}

		internal void NullifyCCHM()
		{
			MyFCHM.NullifyCHM();
			if (MyInsulation != null)
			{
				MyInsulation.FortranInsulation.NullifyFInsulation();
			}
			if (MycSharpFluid != null)
			{
				MycSharpFluid.MyFluidNested.NullifyFluid();
				MycSharpFluid = null;
			}
		}

		internal void InstanciateFluid(int NPhases)
		{
			if (MycSharpFluid == null)
			{
				MycSharpFluid = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid(NPhases);
			}
		}

		internal void InstanciateInsulation(int NLayers)
		{
			if (MyInsulation == null)
			{
				MyInsulation = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation(NLayers);
			}
		}

		internal void PutInsulation(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation RefMyInsulation)
		{
			if (MyInsulation == null)
			{
				MyInsulation = RefMyInsulation;
				return;
			}
			Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.InsulationAlreadySetUp);
			throw ex;
		}

		internal void GetFluid(ref IFlowStream RefOfFluid)
		{
			RefOfFluid = mStream;
		}

		internal unsafe void RestoreFluidCHM()
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid* ptr = MycSharpFluid.ReturnNestedFluid();
			MycSharpFluid.GCAlloc();
			ptr->SetPhasePointer(MycSharpFluid.PhaseHandle.AddrOfPinnedObject());
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid RefToFluid = *ptr;
			MyFCHM.AssociateFluid(ref RefToFluid);
		}

		internal void RestoreInsulation()
		{
			MyInsulation.LoadFInsulationLayer();
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulation RefFInsulation = MyInsulation.FortranInsulation;
			MyFCHM.AssociateInsulaiton(ref RefFInsulation);
		}

		internal void ConnectRightCHM(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM RightCHM)
		{
			MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM = RightCHM;
		}

		internal void GetRightCHM(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM RightCHM)
		{
			RightCHM = MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM;
		}

		internal void ConnectLeftCHM(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM LeftCHM)
		{
			MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM = LeftCHM;
		}

		internal void GetLeftCHM(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM LeftCCHM)
		{
			LeftCCHM = MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM;
		}

		internal void ConnectSideCHM(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM SideCHM)
		{
			MyCConnectivity.MySideCCHM = SideCHM;
		}

		internal void GetSideCHM(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM SideCCHM)
		{
			SideCCHM = MyCConnectivity.MySideCCHM;
		}
	}
	[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 8)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal struct Fluid
	{
		internal IntPtr FortranAddressClone;

		internal IntPtr NestedFLuidAddress;

		private int status;

		private int NumberOfPhases;

		private int flowRegime;

		private IntPtr ArrayAddressClone;

		private IntPtr ArrayAddress;

		private IntPtr ArrayAddressMassClone;

		private IntPtr ArrayAddressMass;

		internal void FluidInit()
		{
			FortranAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
			NestedFLuidAddress = IntPtr.Zero;
			status = -32767;
			NumberOfPhases = -32767;
			flowRegime = 666;
			ArrayAddress = Empty.emptyPtr;
			ArrayAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
			ArrayAddressMass = Empty.emptyPtr;
			ArrayAddressMassClone = IntPtr.Zero;
		}

		internal void SetPhasePointer(IntPtr RefToPhase)
		{
			NestedFLuidAddress = RefToPhase;
		}

		internal int SetStatus(int StatusValue)
		{
			status = StatusValue;
			return 0;
		}

		internal int GetStatus(ref int StatusValue)
		{
			StatusValue = status;
			return 0;
		}

		internal int SetNumberOfPhases(int NumberOfPhasesValue)
		{
			NumberOfPhases = NumberOfPhasesValue;
			return 0;
		}

		internal int GetNumberOfPhases(ref int NumberOfPhasesValue)
		{
			NumberOfPhasesValue = NumberOfPhases;
			return 0;
		}

		internal int SetFlowRegime(int FlowRegimeValue)
		{
			flowRegime = FlowRegimeValue;
			return 0;
		}

		internal int GetFlowRegime(ref int FlowRegimeValue)
		{
			FlowRegimeValue = flowRegime;
			return 0;
		}

		internal void SetArrayAddress(IntPtr iarrayaddress)
		{
			ArrayAddress = iarrayaddress;
		}

		internal void SetArrayAddressMass(IntPtr iarrayaddress)
		{
			ArrayAddressMass = iarrayaddress;
		}

		internal void NullifyFortranAddressClone()
		{
			if (NestedFLuidAddress != IntPtr.Zero)
			{
				NestedFLuidAddress = IntPtr.Zero;
				FortranAddressClone = IntPtr.Zero;
			}
		}

		internal void NullifyFluid()
		{
			NullifyFortranAddressClone();
			SetPhasePointer(IntPtr.Zero);
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal class cSharpFluid
	{
		internal class component
		{
			internal double[] ComponentArray;

			internal component(int Ncomponent)
			{
				ComponentArray = new double[Ncomponent];
			}
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase[] MyPhases;

		internal int NPhases;

		internal int nc;

		private component[] ComponentIndex;

		private component[] ComponentMassIndex;

		internal GCHandle PhaseHandle;

		internal GCHandle[] ArrayHandle;

		internal GCHandle[] ArrayMassHandle;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Fluid MyFluidNested;

		internal int flowRegime;

		internal cSharpFluid()
		{
			MyFluidNested.FluidInit();
			nc = 0;
		}

		internal void CopyUtilityStream(IFlowStream lclStream)
		{
			IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase = null;
			int i = 0;
			if (lclStream.NumberOfPhases > 1)
			{
				for (; i < lclStream.NumberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					hydraulicPhase = lclStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1) as HydraulicPhase;
					MyPhases[i].Cp = hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Cv = hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Density = hydraulicPhase.Density.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Enthalpy = hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value;
					MyPhases[i].FlowRate = hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value;
					MyPhases[i].InternalEnergy = hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value;
					MyPhases[i].mHoldUp = hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Pressure = hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Temperature = hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Velocity = hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Viscosity = hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value;
					MyPhases[i].EqMassFraction = hydraulicPhase.MassFraction.Value;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				hydraulicPhase = lclStream.GetPhaseByID(1) as HydraulicPhase;
				for (; i < 2; i++)
				{
					MyPhases[i].Cp = hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Cv = hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Density = hydraulicPhase.Density.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Enthalpy = hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value;
					MyPhases[i].InternalEnergy = hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Pressure = hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Temperature = hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value;
					MyPhases[i].Viscosity = hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value;
					MyPhases[i].EqMassFraction = hydraulicPhase.MassFraction.Value;
				}
				if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					MyPhases[0].mHoldUp = 1.0;
					MyPhases[0].Velocity = hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value;
					MyPhases[0].FlowRate = hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value;
					MyPhases[1].mHoldUp = 0.0;
					MyPhases[1].Velocity = 0.0;
					MyPhases[1].FlowRate = 0.0;
				}
				if (hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid || hydraulicPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
				{
					MyPhases[0].mHoldUp = 0.0;
					MyPhases[0].Velocity = 0.0;
					MyPhases[0].FlowRate = 0.0;
					MyPhases[1].mHoldUp = 1.0;
					MyPhases[1].Velocity = hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value;
					MyPhases[1].FlowRate = hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value;
				}
			}
			if (ComponentIndex != null && ArrayHandle != null && ComponentMassIndex != null && ArrayMassHandle != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			nc = lclStream.GetPhaseByID(0).NumberOfCompounds;
			ComponentIndex = new component[lclStream.NumberOfPhases + 1];
			ArrayHandle = new GCHandle[lclStream.NumberOfPhases + 1];
			ComponentMassIndex = new component[lclStream.NumberOfPhases + 1];
			ArrayMassHandle = new GCHandle[lclStream.NumberOfPhases + 1];
			hydraulicPhase = lclStream.GetPhaseByID(0) as HydraulicPhase;
			ComponentIndex[0] = new component(nc);
			for (num = 0; num < nc; num++)
			{
				ComponentIndex[0].ComponentArray[num] = hydraulicPhase.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value;
			}
			if (ArrayHandle[0].IsAllocated)
			{
				ArrayHandle[0].Free();
			}
			ArrayHandle[0] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComponentIndex[0].ComponentArray, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			MyFluidNested.SetArrayAddress(ArrayHandle[0].AddrOfPinnedObject());
			ComponentMassIndex[0] = new component(nc);
			for (num = 0; num < nc; num++)
			{
				ComponentMassIndex[0].ComponentArray[num] = hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num].Value;
			}
			if (ArrayMassHandle[0].IsAllocated)
			{
				ArrayMassHandle[0].Free();
			}
			ArrayMassHandle[0] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComponentMassIndex[0].ComponentArray, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			MyFluidNested.SetArrayAddressMass(ArrayMassHandle[0].AddrOfPinnedObject());
			for (i = 0; i < lclStream.NumberOfPhases; i++)
			{
				hydraulicPhase = lclStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1) as HydraulicPhase;
				MyPhases[i].SetNumberOfComponents(nc);
				ComponentIndex[1 + i] = new component(nc);
				ComponentMassIndex[1 + i] = new component(nc);
				for (num = 0; num < nc; num++)
				{
					ComponentIndex[1 + i].ComponentArray[num] = hydraulicPhase.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value;
					ComponentMassIndex[1 + i].ComponentArray[num] = hydraulicPhase.ComponentMassFractions[num].Value;
				}
				if (ArrayHandle[1 + i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ArrayHandle[1 + i].Free();
				}
				ArrayHandle[1 + i] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComponentIndex[1 + i].ComponentArray, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				MyPhases[i].SetConcentrationAddress(ArrayHandle[1 + i].AddrOfPinnedObject());
				if (ArrayMassHandle[1 + i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ArrayMassHandle[1 + i].Free();
				}
				ArrayMassHandle[1 + i] = GCHandle.Alloc(ComponentMassIndex[1 + i].ComponentArray, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				MyPhases[i].SetMassConcentrationAddress(ArrayMassHandle[1 + i].AddrOfPinnedObject());
			}
			GCAlloc();
			int num2 = MyFluidNested.SetNumberOfPhases(lclStream.NumberOfPhases);
			num2 = MyFluidNested.SetStatus(0);
		}

		internal cSharpFluid(int NumberOfPhase)
		{
			MyFluidNested.FluidInit();
			NPhases = NumberOfPhase;
			nc = 0;
			MyPhases = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase[NumberOfPhase];
			int num = 0;
			do
			{
				MyPhases[num] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase(num);
				num++;
			}
			while (num < NumberOfPhase);
			GCAlloc();
			int num2 = MyFluidNested.SetNumberOfPhases(NumberOfPhase);
			num2 = MyFluidNested.SetStatus(0);
		}

		internal void GCAlloc()
		{
			GCFree();
			PhaseHandle = GCHandle.Alloc(MyPhases, GCHandleType.Pinned);
		}

		internal cSharpFluid(int NumberOfPhase, int[] NComponents)
		{
			MyFluidNested.FluidInit();
			NPhases = NumberOfPhase;
			nc = NComponents[0];
			MyPhases = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase[NumberOfPhase];
			int num = 0;
			do
			{
				MyPhases[num] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase(num);
				num++;
			}
			while (num < NumberOfPhase);
			int num2 = MyFluidNested.SetNumberOfPhases(NumberOfPhase);
			num2 = MyFluidNested.SetStatus(0);
			ComponentIndex = new component[NumberOfPhase];
			num = 0;
			do
			{
				ComponentIndex[num] = new component(NComponents[num]);
				num2 = MyPhases[num].SetNumberOfComponents(NComponents[num]);
				num++;
			}
			while (num < NumberOfPhase);
		}

		internal void SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum WhatProperty, int IPhase, double val)
		{
			if (IPhase > GetNumberPhases())
			{
				Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.PhaseIndexGTNumPhases);
				throw ex;
			}
			switch (WhatProperty)
			{
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.AreaOccupiedPerCent:
				SetHoldUp(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cp:
				SetCp(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cv:
				SetCp(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Density:
				SetDensity(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Enthalpy:
				SetEnthalpy(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.ImposedSuperficialVelocity:
				SetImposedSupVelocity(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.InternalEnergy:
				SetInternalEnergy(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Pressure:
				SetPressure(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Temperature:
				SetTemperature(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Velocity:
				SetVelocity(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Viscosity:
				SetViscosity(IPhase, val);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.EqMassFraction:
				SetEqMassFraction(IPhase, val);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid DeepCopy(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.cSharpFluid MyFluid)
		{
			int numberPhases = MyFluid.GetNumberPhases();
			SetNumberOfPhases(numberPhases);
			int i = 0;
			double val = 0.0;
			for (; i < numberPhases; i++)
			{
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.AreaOccupiedPerCent, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.AreaOccupiedPerCent, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cp, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cp, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cv, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cv, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Density, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Density, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Enthalpy, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Enthalpy, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.ImposedSuperficialVelocity, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.ImposedSuperficialVelocity, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.InternalEnergy, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.InternalEnergy, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Pressure, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Pressure, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Temperature, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Temperature, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Velocity, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Velocity, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Viscosity, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.Viscosity, i, val);
				GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.EqMassFraction, i, ref val);
				SetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum.EqMassFraction, i, val);
			}
			return this;
		}

		internal void GetFluid(global::FluidPropertyEnum WhatProperty, int IPhase, ref double val)
		{
			if (IPhase > GetNumberPhases())
			{
				Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.PhaseIndexGTNumPhases);
				throw ex;
			}
			switch (WhatProperty)
			{
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.AreaOccupiedPerCent:
				val = GetHoldUp(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cp:
				val = GetCp(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Cv:
				val = GetCp(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Density:
				val = GetDensity(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Enthalpy:
				val = GetEnthalpy(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.ImposedSuperficialVelocity:
				val = GetImposedSupVelocity(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.InternalEnergy:
				val = GetInternalEnergy(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Pressure:
				val = GetPressure(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Temperature:
				val = GetTemperature(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Velocity:
				val = GetVelocity(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.Viscosity:
				val = GetViscosity(IPhase);
				break;
			case global::FluidPropertyEnum.EqMassFraction:
				val = GetEqMassFraction(IPhase);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void NullifyFluid()
		{
			MyFluidNested.NullifyFluid();
			GCFree();
			GCCompFree();
			GCCompMFree();
		}

		internal void GCFree()
		{
			if (PhaseHandle.IsAllocated)
			{
				PhaseHandle.Free();
			}
		}

		internal void GCCompFree()
		{
			if (ArrayHandle == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < ArrayHandle.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
			{
				if (ArrayHandle[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ArrayHandle[i].Free();
				}
			}
			ArrayHandle = null;
		}

		internal void GCCompMFree()
		{
			if (ArrayMassHandle == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < ArrayMassHandle.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
			{
				if (ArrayMassHandle[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					ArrayMassHandle[i].Free();
				}
			}
			ArrayMassHandle = null;
		}

		private void SetHoldUp(int IPhase, double HoldUp)
		{
			int num = IPhase + 1;
			MyPhases[IPhase].mHoldUp = HoldUp;
		}

		internal void NormaliseHoldUp()
		{
			int num = MyPhases.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			int i = 0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			for (; i < num; i++)
			{
				num2 = MyPhases[i].mHoldUp;
			}
			for (i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				MyPhases[i].mHoldUp = MyPhases[i].mHoldUp / num2;
			}
		}

		internal double GetComponentMoleFractions(int iphase, int icomp)
		{
			return ComponentIndex[iphase].ComponentArray[icomp];
		}

		internal double GetComponentMassFractions(int iphase, int icomp)
		{
			return ComponentMassIndex[iphase].ComponentArray[icomp];
		}

		private double GetHoldUp(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].mHoldUp;
		}

		private void SetVelocity(int IPhase, double Velocity)
		{
			MyPhases[IPhase].Velocity = Velocity;
		}

		private double GetVelocity(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Velocity;
		}

		internal void SetNumberOfPhases(int NumberOfPhase)
		{
			MyPhases = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase[NumberOfPhase];
			NPhases = NumberOfPhase;
			int num = 0;
			do
			{
				MyPhases[num] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Phase(num);
				num++;
			}
			while (num < NumberOfPhase);
			int num2 = MyFluidNested.SetNumberOfPhases(NumberOfPhase);
			num2 = MyFluidNested.SetStatus(0);
			ComponentIndex = new component[NumberOfPhase];
		}

		internal void SetComponentNumber(int ComponentNumber, int IPhase)
		{
			ComponentIndex[IPhase] = new component(ComponentNumber);
			int num = MyPhases[IPhase].SetNumberOfComponents(ComponentNumber);
		}

		private void SetFlowRegime(int value)

			flowRegime = value;
		}

		private int GetFlowRegime()
		{

		}

		private void SetDensity(int IPhase, double Density)
		{
			MyPhases[IPhase].Density = Density;
		}

		private double GetDensity(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Density;
		}

		internal double GetMixtureDensity()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < MyPhases.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
			{
				if (MyPhases[i].mHoldUp < 1E-06)
				{
					throw new Exception(Resources.DivideByZero);
				}
				num = MyPhases[i].Density / MyPhases[i].mHoldUp + num;
			}
			return num;
		}

		internal double GetReynoldsNumber(int IPhase, double diameter)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (MyPhases[IPhase].Viscosity < 1E-07)
			{
				throw new Exception(Resources.ZeroVisc);
			}
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Density * diameter * MyPhases[IPhase].Velocity / MyPhases[IPhase].Viscosity;
		}

		private void SetViscosity(int IPhase, double Viscosity)
		{
			MyPhases[IPhase].Viscosity = Viscosity;
		}

		private double GetViscosity(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Viscosity;
		}

		private void SetPressure(int IPhase, double Pressure)
		{
			MyPhases[IPhase].Pressure = Pressure;
		}

		private double GetPressure(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Pressure;
		}

		private void SetTemperature(int IPhase, double Temperature)
		{
			MyPhases[IPhase].Temperature = Temperature;
		}

		private double GetTemperature(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Temperature;
		}

		private void SetInternalEnergy(int IPhase, double InternalEnergy)
		{
			MyPhases[IPhase].InternalEnergy = InternalEnergy;
		}

		private double GetInternalEnergy(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].InternalEnergy;
		}

		private void SetEnthalpy(int IPhase, double Enthalpy)
		{

		}

		private double GetEnthalpy(int IPhase)
		{
			return MyPhases[IPhase].Enthalpy;
		}

		private void SetCp(int IPhase, double Cp)

		internal int GetNumberPhases()

		}
