	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal class CUOH
	{
		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM[] MyComputationalGrid;

		internal GCHandle[] GCHandleLeft;

		internal GCHandle[] GCHandleRight;

		internal GCHandle[] GCHandleSide;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM this[int par]
		{
			get
			{
				if (par < MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1)
				{
					return MyComputationalGrid[par];
				}
				Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.IndexOutOfBounds);
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		internal CUOH(int NCells, double CellLength, double InternalDiameter, double Inclination, double Roughenss, double InternalTemperature)
		{
			CreateElement(NCells, CellLength, InternalDiameter, Inclination, Roughenss, InternalTemperature);
		}

		internal CUOH(int NElements)
		{
			MyComputationalGrid = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM[NElements];
			for (int i = 0; i < NElements; i++)
			{
				MyComputationalGrid[i] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM();
			}
		}

		internal void GCFree()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < GCHandleLeft.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
			{
				if (GCHandleLeft[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					GCHandleLeft[i].Free();
				}
				if (GCHandleRight[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					GCHandleRight[i].Free();
				}
				if (GCHandleSide[i].IsAllocated)
				{
					GCHandleSide[i].Free();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ComputationalDomainXMLStore(XmlTextWriter tw)
		{
			int i = 0;
			for (int num = MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i < num; i++)
			{
				MyComputationalGrid[i].CHMXMLStore(tw, i);
			}
		}

		internal CUOH(int NCells, double CellLength, double InternalDiameter)
		{
			CreateElement(NCells, CellLength, InternalDiameter, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM GetFirstFace()
		{
			int i = 0;
			for (int num = MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i < num; i++)
			{
				if (MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face)
				{
					return MyComputationalGrid[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM GetFirstCell()
		{
			int i = 0;
			for (int num = MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i < num; i++)
			{
				if (MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)
				{
					return MyComputationalGrid[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM GetNextFace(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM FaceCHM)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM RightCHM = FaceCHM;
			while (FaceCHM != null)
			{
				FaceCHM.GetRightCHM(ref RightCHM);
				if (RightCHM != null && RightCHM.MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face)
				{
					return RightCHM;
				}
				FaceCHM = RightCHM;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM GetNextCell(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM CellCHM)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM RightCHM = CellCHM;
			while (CellCHM != null)
			{
				CellCHM.GetRightCHM(ref RightCHM);
				if (RightCHM != null && RightCHM.MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)
				{
					return RightCHM;
				}
				CellCHM = RightCHM;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal void NullifyCUOH()
		{
			try
			{
				int num = MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
				int num2 = 0;
				do
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[num2].NullifyCCHM();
					num2++;
				}
				while (num2 < num);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string message = ex.Message;
			}
		}

		internal void CreateElement(int NCells, double CellLength, double InternalDiameter, double Inclination, double Roughness, double InternalTemperature)
		{
			MyComputationalGrid = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM[2 * NCells + 1];
			GCHandleRight = new GCHandle[2 * NCells + 1];
			GCHandleLeft = new GCHandle[2 * NCells + 1];
			GCHandleSide = new GCHandle[2 * NCells + 1];
			int i = 0;
			bool flag = true;
			for (; i < 2 * NCells + 1; i++)
			{
				MyComputationalGrid[i] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM();
				if (flag)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face();
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain.Diameter = InternalDiameter;
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain.Inclination = Inclination;
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain.Roughness = Roughness;
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature = InternalTemperature;
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain.DistanceFromBeginningOfUnitOp = (double)(i / 2) * CellLength;
					flag = false;
				}
				else
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell();
					AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell cell = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell();
					cell = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain;
					cell.Lenght = CellLength;
					cell.Diameter = InternalDiameter;
					cell.Diameter = InternalDiameter;
					cell.Inclination = Inclination;
					cell.Roughness = Roughness;
					cell.InternalTemperature = InternalTemperature;
					cell.DistanceFromBeginningOfUnitOp = CellLength / 2.0 + (double)((i - 1) / 2) * CellLength;
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			for (i = 0; i < 2 * NCells; i++)
			{
				MyComputationalGrid[i].ConnectRightCHM(ref MyComputationalGrid[i + 1]);
				GCHandleRight[i] = GCHandle.Alloc(MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
			}
			while (i > 0)
			{
				MyComputationalGrid[i].ConnectLeftCHM(ref MyComputationalGrid[i - 1]);
				GCHandleLeft[i] = GCHandle.Alloc(MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM.MyFCHM, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				i--;
			}
		}

		internal void SetFluid(string WhatProperty, int IPhase, int IComp, double val)
		{
			int num = MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			int num2 = 0;
			IHydraulicPhase hydraulicPhase = MyComputationalGrid[IComp].mStream.GetPhaseByID(IPhase) as HydraulicPhase;
			switch (WhatProperty)
			{
			case "Conductivity":
				hydraulicPhase.Conductivity.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Cp":
				hydraulicPhase.Cp.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Cv":
				hydraulicPhase.Cv.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Density":
				hydraulicPhase.Density.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Enthalpy":
				hydraulicPhase.Enthalpy.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Entropy":
				hydraulicPhase.Entropy.Value = val;
				break;
			case "FlowRate":
				hydraulicPhase.MassFlowRate.Value = val;
				break;
			case "InternalEnergy":
				hydraulicPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = val;
				break;
			case "MolarFraction":
				hydraulicPhase.MolarFraction.Value = val;
				break;
			case "MolecularWeight":
				hydraulicPhase.MolecularWeight.Value = val;
				break;
			case "PerCentAreaOccupied":
				hydraulicPhase.PerCentAreaOccupied.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Pressure":
				hydraulicPhase.Pressure.Value = val;
				break;
			case "SurfaceTension":
				hydraulicPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Temperature":
				hydraulicPhase.Temperature.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Velocity":
				hydraulicPhase.Velocity.Value = val;
				break;
			case "Viscosity":
				hydraulicPhase.Viscosity.Value = val;
				break;
			default:
			{
				Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.NoPropertyInPhase);
				throw ex;
			}
			}
			hydraulicPhase = null;
			num2++;
		}

		internal void RestoreFGeom(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace[] RefMyFFace, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell[] RefMyFCell, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PDiameter[] RefMyPDiameter, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter[] RefMyConstDiameter, GCHandle[] GCRefMyFFace, GCHandle[] GCRefMyFCell, GCHandle[] GCRefMyPDiameter, GCHandle[] GCRefMyConstDiameter)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
			{
				if (MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyFCHM.MyFGeom.SetFGeom(ref MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain, ref RefMyFFace[i], ref RefMyPDiameter[i], ref RefMyConstDiameter[i], GCRefMyFFace[i], GCRefMyPDiameter[i], GCRefMyConstDiameter[i]);
				}
				else if (MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain is AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyFCHM.MyFGeom.SetFGeom(ref MyComputationalGrid[i].MyCompDomain, ref RefMyFCell[i], ref RefMyPDiameter[i], ref RefMyConstDiameter[i], GCRefMyFCell[i], GCRefMyPDiameter[i], GCRefMyConstDiameter[i]);
				}
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void RestoreFCHM(GCHandle gcHandleSummary, FortranMDO[] FixedMDO, int NPhases)
		{
			try
			{
				AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCHM fCHM = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCHM();
				for (int i = 0; i < MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; i++)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[i].InstanciateFluid(NPhases);
					MyComputationalGrid[i].InstanciateInsulation(MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation.NLayers);
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MycSharpFluid.CopyUtilityStream(MyComputationalGrid[i].mStream);
					AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation myInsulation = MyComputationalGrid[i].MyInsulation;
					if (myInsulation.FortranInsulation.EnvirmentTemp.Equals(-32767.0) || myInsulation.FortranInsulation.EnvironmentHTC.Equals(-32767.0))
					{
						myInsulation.CopyStructInsulation(MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation);
					}
					myInsulation.CopyInsulationTemps(MyComputationalGrid[i].mInsulation);
					MyComputationalGrid[i].RestoreFluidCHM();
					MyComputationalGrid[i].RestoreInsulation();
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyFCHM.MyUnitOPSummary = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.UnitOpSummary*)(void*)gcHandleSummary.AddrOfPinnedObject();
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyFCHM.MyMDO = FixedMDO[1];
					MyComputationalGrid[i].MyFCHM.MyPPPIndex = 0;
				}
				RestoreFConnectivity();
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception(string.Format(Resources.MethodFailed, "RestoreCHM"), innerException);
			}
		}

		internal void RestoreFConnectivity()
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (num = 0; num < MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0) + 1; num++)
			{
				if (MyComputationalGrid[num].MyCConnectivity.MyRightCCHM != null)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[num].MyFCHM.AssociateRightCHM(GCHandleRight[num].AddrOfPinnedObject());
				}
			}
			for (num--; num > 0; num--)
			{
				if (MyComputationalGrid[num].MyCConnectivity.MyLeftCCHM != null)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[num].MyFCHM.AssociateLeftCHM(GCHandleLeft[num].AddrOfPinnedObject());
				}
			}
			for (num = 0; num < MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0); num++)
			{
				if (MyComputationalGrid[num].MyCConnectivity.MySideCCHM != null)
				{
					MyComputationalGrid[num].MyFCHM.AssociateSideCHM(GCHandleSide[num].AddrOfPinnedObject());
				}
			}
		}

		internal void RestoreInsulation()
		{
			long num = MyComputationalGrid.GetUpperBound(0);
			int num2 = 0;
			do
			{
				MyComputationalGrid[num2].RestoreInsulation();
				num2++;
			}
			while (num2 < num);
		}

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CCHM loadCHM(int IndexCHM)
		{
			return MyComputationalGrid[IndexCHM];
		}
	}

	[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 8)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	internal struct FGeom
	{
		[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 8)]
		[ComVisible(false)]
		internal struct FGeomTyppe
		{
			internal unsafe AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace* Myface;

			internal unsafe AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell* MyCell;

			internal unsafe void NullifyFGeomType()
			{
				if (Myface != null)
				{
					Myface->NullifyFFace();
				}
				if (MyCell != null)
				{
					MyCell->NullifyFCell();
				}
				Myface = null;
				MyCell = null;
			}
		}

		private int constant;

		private double roughness;

		private double inclination;

		private double sin;

		private double cos;

		private double internal_temperature;

		private FGeomTyppe MyFGeomTyppe;

		internal void NullifyFGeomType()
		{
			MyFGeomTyppe.NullifyFGeomType();
		}

		internal unsafe void SetMyCell(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell* RefMyCell)
		{
			MyFGeomTyppe.MyCell = RefMyCell;
		}

		internal unsafe void SetMyface(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace* RefMyface)
		{
			MyFGeomTyppe.Myface = RefMyface;
		}

		internal unsafe void SetFGeom(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CompDomain MyCompDomain, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace RefMyFFace, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PDiameter RefMyPDiameter, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter RefMyConstDiameter, GCHandle GCRefMyFFace, GCHandle GCRefMyPDiameter, GCHandle GCRefMyConstDiameter)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face face = null;
			face = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Face)MyCompDomain;
			fixed (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace* ptr = &RefMyFFace)
			{
				fixed (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter* ptr2 = &RefMyConstDiameter)
				{
					constant = 1;
					SetMyface((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FFace*)(void*)GCRefMyFFace.AddrOfPinnedObject());
					MyFGeomTyppe.Myface->AssociateDiameter((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter*)(void*)GCRefMyConstDiameter.AddrOfPinnedObject());
					inclination = MyCompDomain.Inclination;
					cos = MyCompDomain.cos;
					sin = MyCompDomain.sin;
					internal_temperature = MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature;
					roughness = MyCompDomain.Roughness;
					MyFGeomTyppe.Myface->SetDiam(face.Diameter);
				}
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void SetFGeom(ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.CompDomain MyCompDomain, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell RefMyFCell, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.PDiameter RefMyPDiameter, ref AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter RefMyConstDiameter, GCHandle GCRefMyFCell, GCHandle GCRefMyPDiameter, GCHandle GCRefMyConstDiameter)
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell cell = null;
			cell = (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.Cell)MyCompDomain;
			fixed (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell* ptr = &RefMyFCell)
			{
				fixed (AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter* ptr2 = &RefMyConstDiameter)
				{
					constant = 1;
					SetMyCell((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FCell*)(void*)GCRefMyFCell.AddrOfPinnedObject());
					MyFGeomTyppe.MyCell->MyPDiameter.AssociateConstDiameter((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.ConstDiameter*)(void*)GCRefMyConstDiameter.AddrOfPinnedObject());
					MyFGeomTyppe.MyCell->SetDiam(cell.Diameter);
					MyFGeomTyppe.MyCell->SetLength(cell.Lenght);
					inclination = MyCompDomain.Inclination;
					cos = MyCompDomain.cos;
					sin = MyCompDomain.sin;
					roughness = MyCompDomain.Roughness;
					internal_temperature = MyCompDomain.InternalTemperature;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	internal interface IFortranInsulation
	{
		AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulation FortranInsulation { get; }

		void LoadFInsulationLayer();
	}
	internal class InternalInsulation
	{
		private AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[] MyFInsulationLayer;

		private AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulation lclFInsulation;

		private GCHandle[] GCInsulation;

		internal int InsualitonLayerCount => MyFInsulationLayer.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;

		internal AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulation FortranInsulation => lclFInsulation;

		internal InternalInsulation()
		{
			lclFInsulation.EnvirmentTemp = -32767.0;
			lclFInsulation.EnvironmentHTC = -32767.0;
			lclFInsulation.InternalDiameter = -32767.0;
			lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls = -32767;
			lclFInsulation.Calculate = -32767;
			GCInsulation = new GCHandle[1];
		}

		internal InternalInsulation(int Nlayers)
		{
			lclFInsulation.EnvirmentTemp = -32767.0;
			lclFInsulation.EnvironmentHTC = -32767.0;
			lclFInsulation.InternalDiameter = -32767.0;
			lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls = -32767;
			lclFInsulation.Calculate = -32767;
			GCInsulation = new GCHandle[1];
			SetNumberOfLayers(Nlayers);
		}

		internal object Clone()
		{
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation internalInsulation = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InternalInsulation();
			internalInsulation.lclFInsulation = lclFInsulation.DeepCopy();
			int num = MyFInsulationLayer.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			internalInsulation.MyFInsulationLayer = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num];
			int num2 = 0;
			do
			{
				internalInsulation.MyFInsulationLayer[num2] = MyFInsulationLayer[num2].DeepCopy();
				num2++;
			}
			while (num2 < num);
			return internalInsulation;
		}

		internal void CopyStructInsulation(IInsulation lclInsulation)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < lclInsulation.NLayers; i++)
			{
				SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.c, i, lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].SpecificHeat.Value);
				SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Conductivity, i, lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].Conductivity.Value);
				SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Density, i, lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].Density.Value);
				SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Heat_Source, i, 0.0);
				if (lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].Thickness.IsUnknown || lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].Thickness.Value < 1E-05)
				{
					SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Thickness, i, 0.025);
				}
				else
				{
					SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Thickness, i, lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].Thickness.Value);
				}
			}
			double externalHT = lclInsulation.ExternalHT;
			if (externalHT >= 0.0 && externalHT < 100000000.0)
			{
				SetGlobalProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationPropertyEnum.EnvironmentHTC, externalHT);
			}
			else
			{
				SetGlobalProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationPropertyEnum.EnvironmentHTC, 10.0);
			}
			if (lclInsulation.ExternalTemperature.IsUnknown)
			{
				SetGlobalProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationPropertyEnum.EnvironmentTemperature, 273.15);
			}
			else
			{
				SetGlobalProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationPropertyEnum.EnvironmentTemperature, lclInsulation.ExternalTemperature.Value);
			}
		}

		internal void CopyInsulationTemps(IInsulation lclInsulation)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < lclInsulation.NLayers; i++)
			{
				SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Temperature, i, lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].InternalTemperature.Value);
				if (i == lclInsulation.NLayers - 1)
				{
					SetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.ExtTemperature, i, lclInsulation.InsulationLayers[i].ExternalTemperature.Value);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InsulationXMLStore(XmlTextWriter tw)
		{
			tw.WriteStartElement("Insulation");
			tw.WriteElementString("EnvirmentTemp", lclFInsulation.EnvirmentTemp.ToString());
			tw.WriteElementString("EnvironmentHTC", lclFInsulation.EnvironmentHTC.ToString());
			tw.WriteElementString("NumberOfWalls", lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls.ToString());
			for (int i = 0; i < InsualitonLayerCount; i++)
			{
				tw.WriteStartElement("InsulationLayer");
				tw.WriteAttributeString("Layer", i.ToString());
				tw.WriteElementString("Conductivity", MyFInsulationLayer[i].Conductivity.ToString());
				tw.WriteElementString("Density", MyFInsulationLayer[i].Density.ToString());
				tw.WriteElementString("Heat_Source", MyFInsulationLayer[i].Heat_Source.ToString());
				tw.WriteElementString("HeatCapacity", MyFInsulationLayer[i].HeatCapacity.ToString());
				tw.WriteElementString("Temperature", MyFInsulationLayer[i].Temperature.ToString());
				tw.WriteElementString("Thickness", MyFInsulationLayer[i].Thickness.ToString());
				tw.WriteEndElement();
			}
			tw.WriteEndElement();
		}

		internal void SetNumberOfLayers(int Nlayers)
		{
			if (lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls == -32767)
			{
				MyFInsulationLayer = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[Nlayers];
				for (int i = 0; i < Nlayers; i++)
				{
					MyFInsulationLayer[i] = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer(-32767.0);
				}
				lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls = Nlayers;
				return;
			}
			Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.NumberOfLayersAlreadySet);
			throw ex;
		}

		internal void AddLayer()
		{
			int num = MyFInsulationLayer.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[] array = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num];
			array = MyFInsulationLayer;
			MyFInsulationLayer = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num + 1];
			int num2 = 0;
			do
			{
				MyFInsulationLayer[num2] = array[num2];
				num2++;
			}
			while (num2 < num);
			lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls = num + 1;
		}

		internal void AddLayer(int ILayer)
		{
			int num = MyFInsulationLayer.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[] array = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num];
			array = MyFInsulationLayer;
			MyFInsulationLayer = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num + 1];
			long num2 = 0L;
			do
			{
				MyFInsulationLayer[num2] = array[num2];
				num2++;
			}
			while (num2 < ILayer);
			num2++;
			do
			{
				MyFInsulationLayer[num2] = array[num2 - 1];
				num2++;
			}
			while (num2 < num + 1);
			lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls = num + 1;
		}

		internal void DeleteLayer(int ILayer)
		{
			long num = 0L;
			int num2 = MyFInsulationLayer.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[] array = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num2];
			do
			{
				array[num] = MyFInsulationLayer[num];
				num++;
			}
			while (num < num2);
			num2--;
			MyFInsulationLayer = new AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer[num2];
			num = 0L;
			do
			{
				MyFInsulationLayer[num] = array[num];
				num++;
			}
			while (num < ILayer);
			if (num < num2)
			{
				do
				{
					MyFInsulationLayer[num] = array[num + 1];
					num++;
				}
				while (num < num2);
			}
			lclFInsulation.NumberOfWalls = num2 + 1;
		}

		{
			switch (WhichProperty)

			case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationPropertyEnum.InternalDiameter:

					break;

					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.ExtTemperature:

					break;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void GetLayerProperty(AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty WhichProperty, ref double[] Val)
		{
			int upperBound = MyFInsulationLayer.GetUpperBound(0);
			int upperBound2 = Val.GetUpperBound(0);
			if (upperBound2 != upperBound)
			{
				Exception ex = new Exception(Resources.ArrayDimDifferent);
				throw ex;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < upperBound; i++)
			{
				switch (WhichProperty)
				{
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Temperature:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].Temperature;
					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.c:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].HeatCapacity;
					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Conductivity:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].Conductivity;
					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Thickness:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].Thickness;
					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Density:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].Density;
					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.Heat_Source:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].Heat_Source;
					break;
				case AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.InsulationLayerProperty.ExtTemperature:
					Val[i] = MyFInsulationLayer[i].ExtTemperature;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void LoadFInsulationLayer()
		{
			if (!GCInsulation[0].IsAllocated)
			{
				GCInsulation[0] = GCHandle.Alloc(MyFInsulationLayer, GCHandleType.Pinned);
				lclFInsulation.setInsualitonPointer((AspenTech.Hydraulics.Framework.FInsulationLayer*)(void*)GCInsulation[0].AddrOfPinnedObject());
			}
		}

		internal void DeallocateFInsulation()
		{
			if (GCInsulation[0].IsAllocated)
			{
				GCInsulation[0].Free();
			}
		}

		internal unsafe void NullifyInsulationPointer()
		{
			lclFInsulation.setInsualitonPointer(null);
		}
	}

		internal double Pressure;

		internal double InternalEnergy;

		internal double Cv;

		internal int dirty;

		internal int ArrayDimension;

		internal IntPtr ArrayFortranClone;

		internal IntPtr ArrayAddress;

		internal IntPtr ArrayFortranMassClone;

		internal IntPtr ArrayAddressMass;

		internal double DF_DP;

		{
			mHoldUp = -32767.0;
			mHeight = -32767.0;
			Velocity = -32767.0;
			FlowRate = -32767.0;
			Density = -32767.0;
			Viscosity = -32767.0;
			EqMassFraction = -32767.0;
			Pressure = -32767.0;
			Temperature = -32767.0;
			Enthalpy = -32767.0;
			InternalEnergy = -32767.0;
			Cp = -32767.0;
			Cv = -32767.0;
			dirty = 0;
			ArrayDimension = -32767;
			ArrayFortranClone = IntPtr.Zero;
			ArrayFortranMassClone = IntPtr.Zero;
			ArrayAddress = IntPtr.Zero;
			ArrayAddressMass = IntPtr.Zero;
			DF_DP = 0.0;
		}

		}

		}

		{

			return 0;
		}

				lclOutletDiameter = OutletDiameter;
			}

				lclThroatDiameter = ThroatDiameter;
			}
		}

		{
